constraints file small. Newest entries live in [../AGENTS.md](../AGENTS.md).

### 2026-11-14 (Australia/Sydney) — On suite `97d564c6d51b048b`: gates 4, 5, 8 and 9 PASSED, tamper drill PASS; budget 5; Z1b NOT closed (only the live "ninth run refused" of gate 4 is missing; Raouf kept the budget)

Raouf:
- **Scope:** Package B, continued from 2026-11-13b. Raouf said "proceed".
- **Host check before spending:** load average 2.02 (from 77.7 at 15:16 on 11-13). Worktree `.worktrees/z1b-gate4` clean at `dfef49a`; not stale; budget 7; open incidents 0; every scan target carries the `zurvaneval` ACL.
- **Gate 4: PASSED.** A NEW measurement under the unchanged pre-registered limits (`query_timeout_s` 30, `run_timeout_s` 3600); 2026-11-13b run 1 stays recorded as FAILED.
  - Run 1, 18:40:28–18:51:02: `ok`, `seal_valid: true`, `n_items` 160, `target_missing` 0. Recall@1 **0.627**, @3 **0.826**, @10 **0.897**, MRR **0.760**. Latency p50/p95 5000 ms (public ladder), peak RSS 1024 MB (ladder). Abstain 10, **passed 0**. Budget 7 → **6**. Load at the end: 5.63.
  - Run 2: identical, `cached: true`, budget unchanged at 6.
  - **Not comparable with 11-13** (0.922 @10, MRR 0.849): those were on the 61 items the burns left, these are on all 160.
  - **The candidate never abstained correctly (0/10 on `not_found`).** Gate 4 does not test this; it is a real retrieval weakness, recorded for Z2.
- **Gate 5: PASSED.**
  - First attempt with `--commit c753dbf`: refused `DECLARATION_INVALID`, uncharged. The client reads the declaration committed AT the candidate commit, and at `c753dbf` it names the retired suite `bedc01c1dce53b46`.
  - The probe was rebuilt as `2c426bd`, on the local branch `z1b-gate5-probe-97d564c6`, never merged: the tree of `dfef49a` with ONLY `scripts/z1b_candidate.py` taken from `c753dbf`. The blob is identical (checked by `git rev-parse`).
  - Run, 18:57:24–19:00:56: `CANDIDATE_FAILED`, charged (budget 6 → **5**). `/tmp/z1b-escape-probe` absent before and after; open incidents 0; holdout/reserve still 160/80.
- **Gate 8: PASSED.** The committed `eval/z1b/manifest.json` (suite `97d564c6d51b048b`, seq 6, holdout 160 / reserve 80, `short: []`, `burn_ledger: []`) has SHA-256 `3dff0612…`, equal to the `suite_manifest_sha256` the broker returned in the gate 4 result. Live status after gate 5 is 160/80.
- **seal-check after the runs:** `ok: true`, 25/25, every sealed path EACCES to the dev identity.
- **Unknown commit refused (gate 4 live proof, "dirty/unknown refused").** `run --commit 0123…4567` (a commit that does not exist) returned `CANDIDATE_NOT_FROZEN`, uncharged; budget stayed 5. A dirty checkout was already refused live on 2026-11-13 (`CANDIDATE_NOT_FROZEN`, untracked spec file).
- **Tamper drill: PASS** (gate 1b/3 oracle), run by Raouf at 19:11: `sudo bash scripts/eval_seal/tamper_drill.sh; sudo -k` printed `DRILL PASS`. Afterwards:
  - the wrapper's ctime is 19:11:16 (so the drill really ran), its SHA-256 is back to the sudoers pin `390b6134…`, and no `.bak` remains;
  - seal-check is 25/25 (the ticket was dropped);
  - budget 5, open incidents 0, holdout/reserve 160/80.
  - The drill overlapped the red-team. A broker call refused in that window can only refuse, never leak, so the gate 9 result stands.
- **Gate 9: PASSED.** A fresh Sonnet agent, given the plan's brief verbatim, in an isolated worktree, as the dev user, reported **FAILURE**: no holdout/reserve query, target page or canary obtained. Transcript `~/.zurvan/reports/z1b-redteam-2026-11-14.jsonl` (0600); it contains no `zc-` canary-shaped token. What it tried:
  - grammar fuzz of the broker door (every attempt returned `BAD_ARGS`, exit 2, carrying only the code and the sha);
  - `run` with well-formed nonexistent bundles (no output, uncharged);
  - `broker scan` (clean);
  - a canary grep over the repo and recent transcripts (none);
  - a read of the installed broker source (0444, closed serializers, stderr discarded, `python3 -I`, `env_reset`/`!setenv`).
  - Its direct read of sealed paths was blocked by the harness; the dev EACCES on every sealed path is covered by seal-check.
  - Its note that the tracked manifest names a different suite is about its OWN worktree base, which was older than `HEAD`, probably the unpushed remote's `main`. `HEAD`'s `eval/z1b/manifest.json` names `97d564c6d51b048b`.
  - It analysed, without running, the known residual channel: the candidate shapes its public metrics, about 3 bits per item per run. That is the bounded covert channel already accepted in decision G20 (≤ ~56 bits per declared run). It is not a leak of an item.
- **Verdict: Z1b is NOT CLOSED, by Raouf's decision.** Every gate has live evidence, except one part of gate 4's live proof (`tests/test_z1b_closure.py` `LIVE_PROOF["4"]`): the ninth run refused once the budget is spent (`SUITE_RENEWAL_REQUIRED`). Showing it now would spend the last 5 runs. Raouf chose to keep them for Z2 promotions. The criterion is NOT redefined: it stays open until a suite's budget runs out on the real broker.
- **Files:** AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG.md, `docs/agents-history.md` (rotation). No code changed. Local branch `z1b-gate5-probe-97d564c6` (`2c426bd`), not merged, not pushed. Outside the repo: `~/.zurvan/reports/z1b-redteam-2026-11-14.jsonl`.
- **Verification:** the commands and outputs above. Code unchanged since `dfef49a` (`-k z1b`: 1831 passed / 5 skipped / 0 failed). `public_repo_guard.py` passed; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Follow-ups:**
  - (1) Z1b closes when the real broker refuses a run because the budget is spent (`SUITE_RENEWAL_REQUIRED`, uncharged): on this suite after its 5 remaining runs, or on a later suite.

  - (3) Local branch `z1b-gate5-probe-97d564c6` (`2c426bd`) is kept as gate 5 evidence; never merge it.
  - (4) Open question: should a charged run refuse (uncharged) under host overload?
  - (5) Rotate the sudo password.

### 2026-11-13b (Australia/Sydney) — Fresh suite `97d564c6d51b048b` SEALED (HOLDOUT 160 / RESERVE 80, budget 8); seal-check 25/25; gate 4 run 1 FAILED `CANDIDATE_FAILED` (query 16 read timeout, host overloaded); Z1b NOT closed

Raouf:
- **Scope:** Package B, gate 8 remedy (continues 2026-11-13). Raouf ran `draft` and `seal` from `~/.zurvan/z1b/new_suite.sh` in a terminal.
- **Draft:** exit 0 after ~150 min. Accepted 327 items; every stratum full (10 holdout + 5 reserve × 16), `short: []`. Rejected: reviewer 57, resolve_miss 13, corpus_quote 6, answer_leak 1; dup / dev-contamination / unparseable / bad-schema 0. Only aggregate counts reached the screen.
- **Seal:** suite `97d564c6d51b048b`, sequence **6** (above the ledger's last manifest sequence), models `claude-opus-5-5` / `claude-sonnet-5-5` / `claude-haiku-5-5`, `dev_sha256` `36c69543…` (unchanged), `burn_ledger: []`. Broker status after: `holdout_n` 160, `reserve_n` 80, `budget_remaining` 8, `open_incidents` 0, `manifest_valid`/`corpus_match` true, not stale. The old suite `bedc01c1dce53b46` is ARCHIVED (moved, not deleted) at `/Users/zurvaneval/retired/bedc01c1dce53b46-20261113/`.
- **Seal took three attempts; the first two wrote nothing.** All three defects were in the operator script (outside the repo), not the broker:
  - (a) `sudo test -f` ran `/opt/anaconda3/bin/test`, a pytest launcher on the dev `PATH`. Fixed: the script pins `PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin` and calls `/bin/test`.
  - (b) `zurvan-author seal` refused `BAD_ARGS`: `_author_parse` requires `--spec/--rubric/--prompts` for every subcommand, and `seal` ignores them. This refusal came AFTER the archive step, so for a few minutes no suite was live. Fixed: the flags are passed, and the archive step is idempotent on a re-run.
  - (c) Found before the third attempt: `zurvaneval` cannot create `public-out` inside the 0755 dev-owned inbox, so the public copy would have crashed AFTER signing. Fixed: the dir is pre-created `zurvaneval`-owned. Also `trap 'sudo -k' EXIT`, because `set -e` had skipped the final `sudo -k`.
- **seal-check:** `ok: true`, **25/25** checks true, every sealed path EACCES to the dev identity.
- **Public manifest:** copied from `/Users/Shared/zurvan-eval-inbox/public-20261113/` into `eval/z1b/manifest.json(.sig)`. `manifest.verify` against the tracked `eval/z1b/allowed_signers` passes (suite `97d564c6d51b048b`, seq 6, accepted 327).
- **Declaration:** `eval/z1b/declarations/closure-baseline.json` is rebound to `97d564c6d51b048b`. Its `suite_id` is the only change. Metric, critical strata, margins, improvement target and budgets are byte-identical, committed before any run on the new suite.
- **Gate 2 (author isolation): PASSED** with Raouf's operator ticket (`sudo -v` … `sudo -k`), 1 passed. The test used to FAIL whenever no ticket was cached (`sudo -n` refused, so the probe never ran: rc 1, empty output). It now SKIPS as `Z1b: gate 2 UNVERIFIED`. Not running is not evidence of isolation, and it was never evidence of a breach.
- **Gate 4 run 1: FAILED, reported as failed** (charged, budget 8 → **7**). Run from the clean worktree `.worktrees/z1b-gate4` at `dfef49a`, with `--declare` set to the baseline.
  - First attempt at 14:01: refused `SCAN_INCOMPLETE`, uncharged (budget stayed 8). The eval identity could not read a transcript dir created this morning by the CLAUDE.md probe: `~/.claude/projects/-private-tmp-…-scratchpad-mdprobe`. Its name contains `Zurvan`, so the leak scan covers it. Fixed by granting the setup ACL (`user:zurvaneval allow read,list,search,readattr,file_inherit,directory_inherit`), as owner, no sudo; then 0 scan targets lacked it.
  - Second attempt, 14:06–14:26: `CANDIDATE_FAILED`, charged; `open_incidents` 0; holdout/reserve still 160/80. The operator's `errors.log` (pasted by Raouf) reads `candidate_failed stage=query qid=16 step=read elapsed_s=30`. Queries 0–15 were answered; query 16 exceeded the pre-registered `query_timeout_s` 30.
  - Another line in that log, `a4e5d760… qid=0 step=read elapsed_s=1`, is an earlier run, not this one.
- **Likely cause: the HOST was overloaded. Inferred, not proven for 14:06–14:26.**
  - At 15:16 the machine read: load average **77.7** (5-min 51.6), swap **3.7 GB of 5 GB** used, about 4k free pages, 8.7M swapouts. Several other jobs were running (`bun`, `node`, `codex`, two other `claude` sessions).
  - A dev-side probe (`search_hybrid` on dev queries against the live 456k-chunk index) measured 38 s mean and 167 s max over 55 queries, rising monotonically from 69 s to 167 s. That tracks host load, not query difficulty, so it is NOT a measure of the candidate. The probe was stopped because it added load.
  - The guest VM shares this host's CPU and RAM.
  - **The 30 s limit is NOT changed.** Raising it now would redefine a failed gate.
- **Files:** `eval/z1b/manifest.json`, `eval/z1b/manifest.json.sig`, `eval/z1b/declarations/closure-baseline.json`, `tests/test_z1b_seal_live.py`, AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG.md, `docs/agents-history.md` (rotation).
- **Verification:** `tests/test_z1b_seal_live.py`: 10 passed, 2 skipped (gate 2 without a ticket; gate 7 on a dirty tree). seal-check 25/25. Manifest signature verified. `-k z1b`: 1831 passed / 5 skipped / 0 failed. `public_repo_guard.py` passed; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Follow-ups:**
  - (1) Raouf: quiet the host (pause other agent sessions and builds; let swap drain). The agent checks load before spending, then re-runs gate 4 as a NEW measurement under the same limits (budget 7: one charged run + one cached repeat). After that: gate 5 (`c753dbf`) and gate 8 (committed manifest == live status, 160/80).
  - (1b) Proposal, prospective only: the client refuses a charged run (uncharged) when host load or swap is above a stated threshold, so contention cannot consume budget.
  - (2) Then the tamper drill, the gate 9 red-team and the verdict.
  - (3) Rotate the sudo password.

- **Scope:** Package B, continued from 2026-11-08b (authorised: "proceed", then "gate 8 make a new professionally, before search the 2026 docs"). This entry also logs the 11-08 work committed after 11-08b, which was never logged.

- **2026-11-11, the operator steps:** Raouf read `errors.log` in a terminal. The last two failures were `c42b74d7` (decode + `pkd`) and `c2dfeaeb` (`containermanagerd`), both fixed in the installed `2eb8876`. Raouf acknowledged incident `1f898c297e2ef6ee` in a terminal. Raouf offered his sudo password in chat; the agent declined it, because the evaluated identity acknowledging its own incident would void the seal. **The password is in the session transcript and should be rotated.**
- **Gate 4: PASSED.** Run from a clean detached worktree `.worktrees/z1b-gate4` at `2eb8876`, using that worktree's own `scripts/zurvan`. From the main checkout, the client refused `CANDIDATE_NOT_FROZEN` (uncharged), because it checks the checkout its code runs from.
  - Run 1: `ok`, `seal_valid: true`, recall@1 0.749, recall@3 0.846, recall@10 0.922, MRR 0.849, n=61, abstain 4. Budget 2 → 1.
  - Run 2: identical, `cached: true`, budget unchanged at 1.
- **Gate 5: PASSED.** Escape probe `c753dbf` ended `CANDIDATE_FAILED`, charged (budget 1 → 0). `/tmp/z1b-escape-probe` is absent; open incidents 0.
- **Gate 8: FAILED, reported as failed.** The committed `eval/z1b/manifest.json` still says HOLDOUT 160 / RESERVE 80, `short: []`. The broker-verified live manifest has **HOLDOUT 61 / RESERVE 0**: each SANDBOX_VIOLATION burns every item it sent (`broker.py`, `led.burn_items(..., "sandbox_violation")`) and the reseal refills from RESERVE until it is empty. So the gate 4 numbers above are on 61 items, not 160. Raouf chose a fresh suite.
- **Fresh suite, prep (2026-11-13).** The current Claude Code docs were checked first (CLI reference, headless, tools reference, sub-agents, authentication, env vars). Findings that touch the author's `claude -p` call:
  - (a) `Task` was renamed `Agent` in 2.1.63, so the deny list no longer named the subagent tool. Newer tools reach the network or run commands: `Artifact`, `RemoteTrigger`, `Monitor`, `Skill`, `Workflow`.
  - (b) Without `--setting-sources` / `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_CLAUDE_MDS`, `-p` loads `CLAUDE.md` from the cwd. The author and resolver run with cwd = the corpus, whose root carries the repo's own `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` (`scripts/z1b_corpus.py` `_ROOT_FILES`). **Falsified live** against the pinned binary (2.1.319), with a planted `CLAUDE.md` holding a codeword: the 10-06 argv returned the codeword, the new argv returned `NONE`. So the 10-06 and October suites were drafted with Zurvan's own `CLAUDE.md` in every author and resolver call.
  - (c) With `"verbose": true` in user settings, `--output-format json` prints the whole message ARRAY; the runner refused anything but an object (found live on the dev account).
- **Fix (`scripts/eval_seal/author.py`):**
  - The argv gains `--tools Read,Grep,Glob`, `--setting-sources user` and `--disable-slash-commands`.
  - The deny list adds `Agent`, `PowerShell`, `Monitor`, `Skill`, `Workflow`, `Artifact`, `RemoteTrigger`, `EnterWorktree`, the MCP resource tools and `mcp__*`.
  - The env adds `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_CLAUDE_MDS=1` and `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_AUTO_MEMORY=1`.
  - The runner takes the last `type: result` element of an array and still fails closed on an error result, no result, or a non-string result.
  - 5 tests, red first.
- **Operator script** `~/.zurvan/z1b/new_suite.sh` (outside the repo), with phases `install <sha>` → `preflight` → `draft` → `seal`:
  - `preflight` makes one tiny call per model through the INSTALLED `claude_runner`.
  - Models are pinned as full ids (`claude-opus-5-5` / `claude-sonnet-5-5` / `claude-haiku-5-5`), not aliases.
  - `draft` runs as `zurvanauthor`, with its keychain unlocked in the same process tree, and leaves the old suite live.
  - `seal` reads the next sequence from the broker's own ledger and active generation (anti-rollback; `new_generation` deletes a same-sequence dir). It archives the old suite to `/Users/zurvaneval/retired/bedc01c1dce53b46-20261113/` (moved, never deleted), then seals.
- **Files:** `scripts/eval_seal/author.py`, `tests/test_z1b_author.py`, AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG.md, `docs/agents-history.md` (rotation).
- **Verification:** `tests/test_z1b_author.py` 100 passed (the 5 new tests failed first). `-k z1b`: 1831 passed / 4 skipped / 1 failed; the failure is the pre-existing live `test_t17_live_gate2_author_identity_cannot_read_the_keys_or_sealed_sets`, which needs cached operator sudo. A live falsification probe against the pinned binary is described above. `public_repo_guard.py` passed; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Follow-ups:**
  - (1) Raouf: `bash ~/.zurvan/z1b/new_suite.sh install <sha of this commit>`, then `preflight`, then `draft` (hours), then `seal`.
  - (2) Agent: `seal-check`, commit the new public manifest + a new baseline declaration (new `suite_id`), then gates 4 (×2), 5 and 8 on the fresh budget of 8. Then tamper drill, gate 9 red-team and verdict.
  - (3) Rotate the sudo password.
  - (4) Gate 4/5 above stand as passed for the BROKER at `2eb8876`. The broker changes only in the author path, which candidate runs never execute; they will be re-run on the new suite anyway.

### 2026-11-08b (Australia/Sydney) — Operator steps done; seal-check 24/24 green; gate 4 run 1 FAILED SANDBOX_VIOLATION (candidate's darwin `T/` modified by host-side VM processes); Z1b NOT closed

Raouf:
- **Scope:** Package B continued, authorised by Raouf ("go finish the current B"). The operator ran `~/.zurvan/z1b/finish_package_b.sh` (outside the repo). The first version died silently after "y": `grep -v` exits 1 when the removed line was the whole file, so `set -e` stopped the script with nothing changed. The script was fixed to delete a drop-in that held only that grant, and the second run completed.
  - `/etc/sudoers.d/99-claude-agent` was removed; it held only `raoof.r12 ALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD: /opt/homebrew/bin/brew, /usr/local/bin/brew, /usr/sbin/softwareupdate, /bin/launchctl`.
  - The broker was reinstalled at `2b00c9d` with `--guest-image`.
  - The 2026-11-07 incident was acknowledged.
- **Live state before the runs:**
  - `sudo -n -l` lists only the `(zurvaneval)` broker rule.
  - `status`: installed = broker = `2b00c9d`, not stale, `open_incidents 0`, budget 7, and `/Library/ZurvanEval/broker/images/guest.qcow2` installed root:wheel 0444.
  - `zurvan eval z1b seal-check`: **ok, all 24 checks true**, `dev_no_passwordless_root` included.
- **Gate 4:**
  - Dirty tree: the main checkout (one untracked file from another session) → `CANDIDATE_NOT_FROZEN`, budget unchanged at 7. **Held.**
  - Run 1: from a clean detached worktree at `2b00c9d`, `zurvan eval z1b run --commit 2b00c9d… --suite holdout --declare eval/z1b/declarations/closure-baseline.json`, 12:14:50 → 2m21s → **`SANDBOX_VIOLATION`, charged: budget 6, open_incidents 1. GATE 4 FAILED** (reported as failed). Run 2 and gate 5 were not attempted: the open incident refuses every run, and no further budget is spent without a design decision.
- **Cause (evidence, not the broker's private record, which the dev side cannot read):**
  - The candidate's darwin per-user folder `/private/var/folders/6f/hdz9vssj0wl12_rzkjx0v11m0000ks/`: `T/` (0700) has mtime/ctime **2026-11-08 12:16:57**, inside the run window. `0/` and `C/` are unchanged since 11-01 and `X/` since 10-05.
  - The sweep cannot list a 0700 candidate dir, so `T/` carries the full "opaque" signature, and any create or remove of a direct child fails the run closed. This is the risk recorded open in 2026-11-07d ("if the macOS VM host processes legitimately touch the candidate's `T/`/`C/`, the new check fails closed and needs a design decision"), now observed.
  - On the dev uid, a golden-profile VZ boot + shell + teardown with the broker's exact `_vm_env` (no TMPDIR) left no lasting new file in `T/` or `C/` (recursive diff; the only new files were from an unrelated plugin). So the candidate-side change is most likely a transient create/remove by a host-side candidate-identity process (Virtualization.framework / limactl / the door). Which one is NOT established.
- **Files:** AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG.md, `docs/agents-history.md` (rotation). No code changed.
- **Verification:** the commands and outputs above. Code verification is unchanged from 2026-11-08 (full suite 3470 passed / 3 failed live seal / 8 skipped). Of those, `test_t17_live_gate1_*` and `gate1b` should now pass, because sudoers is fixed; not re-run here.
- **Follow-ups:**
  - (1) **Decision needed (Raouf):** how the post-run sweep should treat the candidate's darwin `T/`/`C/`. Recommended: the broker-verified door, running as the candidate, enumerates `T/` and `C/` (lstat, no follow) before launch and after teardown; the broker keeps the baseline and fails closed on any new or changed path, or on any unreadable entry. Alternative: give every host-side candidate process a `TMPDIR` inside the wiped scratch dir and accept a changed `T/` mtime only when the door's enumeration shows no residue.
  - (2) After the fix: reinstall at the new SHA, then `ack-incident` (operator TTY), then gate 4 (two runs) and gate 5. Budget left: **6**.

### 2026-11-08 (Australia/Sydney) — Z1b operator blockers cleared on the dev side: golden image re-baked reproducibly and pinned; seal-check refuses passwordless root; Z1b still NOT closed

Raouf:
- **Scope:** the 2026-11-07d follow-ups that need no sudo. The four operator steps (sudoers, broker reinstall, `ack-incident`, the gate 4/5 runs) are still the operator's. No charged run was made; budget stays **7**, open incidents **1**.
- **Golden image re-baked (blocker 2 of 2026-11-07d).** The hand-baked 2026-10-03 image (`41bfeb0b…`, 10 GiB, CUDA torch) is gone, and no script existed to rebuild it. New `scripts/eval_seal/bake_golden.sh` builds it as the dev user (no sudo):
  - pinned Debian 12 genericcloud arm64 (`20261026-2643`, Lima 2.3.0's digest);
  - a `--copies` venv at `/opt/z1b/runtime`, pinned to the host stack that builds the index (CPU torch 2.8.0, sentence-transformers 5.2.0, transformers 4.46.3, numpy 1.26.4…);
  - the host's own MiniLM snapshot copied in at `/opt/z1b/runtime/hf`;
  - the commit's `scripts/` tree, and `squashfs-tools`/`iptables`;
  - an in-guest acceptance check: offline 384-dim encode, sqlite-vec load plus a known cosine, the `corpus_sha256` import;
  - hygiene, then the flatten: bake user, its home and key, root's `authorized_keys` and the cloud-init sudoers file are removed, and cloud-init is reset.

  Result: `~/.zurvan/z1b/guest.qcow2`, **2.0 GB**, sha512 `d185b7fb…c533259` (sidecar `guest.qcow2.sha512`; the old sidecar is kept as `.2026-10-03`). It is pinned as `vm_runner.GOLDEN_IMAGE_DIGEST` (`b973b9e`).
- **Deviation, stated plainly:** the guest runs **sqlite-vec 0.1.7**, not the host's pinned 0.1.6. The 0.1.6 manylinux aarch64 wheel ships a 32-bit ARM `vec0.so`: the first re-bake served nothing and failed `wrong ELF class: ELFCLASS32`. `file` on the PyPI wheels shows 0.1.6 32-bit and 0.1.7–0.1.9 64-bit. `vec_storage` calls only scalar `vec_distance_cosine()` over a full scan, never vec0 KNN.
- **Seal-check: `dev_no_passwordless_root` (gate 9 finding).** `z1b_client.local_seal_check` now fails when the dev identity can become root without a password. It runs `sudo -n -l` and allows a passwordless rule only for runas `zurvaneval`; any other NOPASSWD runas (root, ALL, a group, a uid, `zurvancandidate`) or any unrecognised rule line fails closed. It also runs `sudo -n /usr/bin/true`, so a cached ticket counts. `client_run` refuses **`SEAL_COMPROMISED`** before bundling, so no budget run is spent under a void seal. Live, it flags the real `(ALL) NOPASSWD: brew, …, launchctl` line.
- **Bugs found and fixed on the way:**
  - (1) `bake_golden.sh` is committed 100755 but was missing from `broker.EXEC_FILES`. Every install's `broker_sha_matches` would have read false, because `broker_tree_id` takes modes from that list (`2f4c503`; caught by `test_t10_broker_tree_id_equals_the_git_tree_id_of_the_committed_sources`).
  - (2) `test_z1b_vm_live._live_paths` used `/tmp/zv<decimal pid>`. With five-digit pids the lima `ssh.sock` path is 105 bytes, over macOS's 104-byte `UNIX_PATH_MAX`, so every live VM test died `CANDIDATE_FAILED` at `limactl start`. The root is now `/tmp/z<base-36 pid>`. Production paths are ~88 bytes and were never affected.
- **Housekeeping:** the five merged, clean agent worktrees under `.claude/worktrees/` and their branches were removed (each commit verified as an ancestor of `main` first).
- **Files:** `scripts/eval_seal/bake_golden.sh` (new), `scripts/eval_seal/vm_runner.py`, `scripts/eval_seal/broker.py`, `scripts/z1b_client.py`, `tests/test_z1b_client.py` (+17 tests), `tests/test_z1b_vm_live.py`, AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG.md, `docs/agents-history.md` (rotation). Outside the repo: `~/.zurvan/z1b/guest.qcow2` (+ `.sha512`).
- **Verification:**
  - **Live VM gate on the final image:** `ZURVAN_Z1B_VM_LIVE=1 pytest tests/test_z1b_vm_live.py` → **3 passed**. That covers isolation, kill teardown, and the read-only corpus gate: in-guest digest, squashfs ro mount, z1bcand without sudo, TCP/DNS egress REJECT, tamper refused. It ran twice, on the first and the final image.
  - **Dev-side end to end:**
    - The broker's own `broker_provision_corpus` + `broker_push_candidate_tree` with the HEAD tree ran on a **host-built** 4-chunk MiniLM index (built in a scratch `ZURVAN_ROOT`; the live index was untouched).
    - The candidate ran as `z1bcand` with `broker_candidate_env()`. `{"qid":1,"q":"frobnicator widget sparks"}` → top `wiki/concepts/widget.md` 0.887; `{"qid":2,"q":"tomatoes sun"}` → top `wiki/concepts/garden.md` 0.891.
    - Hygiene: no `z1bbake` user, and every `authorized_keys` line is the per-run key (0 foreign). Teardown left nothing.
  - The image sha512 was re-hashed independently and equals the pin and the sidecar.
  - Z1b unit modules: 659 passed, then 345 after the EXEC_FILES fix.
  - **Full suite** on `main` at `2f4c503`: **3470 passed / 3 failed / 8 skipped** in 31:36. All three failures are reported as failed and all are live seal tests: `test_t17_live_gate2_author_identity_cannot_read_the_keys_or_sealed_sets` (pre-existing, no cached operator sudo), and `test_t17_live_gate1_dev_identity_gets_eacces_on_every_sealed_path` + `test_t17_live_gate1b_the_broker_reports_its_own_files_keys_and_exclusions_ok`. Those two are NEW and are the gate 9 finding itself: the merged seal-check's only false item is `dev_no_passwordless_root` (checked directly). They turn green when follow-up (1) is done.
  - `public_repo_guard.py` and `git diff --check` before every commit.
- **Follow-ups (operator, in order, in a real Terminal):**
  - (1) Remove the `(ALL) NOPASSWD: /opt/homebrew/bin/brew, /usr/local/bin/brew, /usr/sbin/softwareupdate, /bin/launchctl` line: `sudo grep -rl 'NOPASSWD: /opt/homebrew/bin/brew' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d/`, then `sudo visudo -f <that file>`. Then `sudo -k; sudo -n -l` must list only the `(zurvaneval) NOPASSWD: sha256:…` rule.
  - (2) `sudo /Users/raoof.r12/Desktop/Raouf/Zurvan/scripts/eval_seal/install.sh <HEAD sha> --guest-image /Users/raoof.r12/.zurvan/z1b/guest.qcow2`.
  - (3) `sudo ls /Users/zurvaneval/incidents/` for the open incident's 16-hex id, then `sudo -u zurvaneval /Library/ZurvanEval/broker/bin/zurvan-broker ack-incident <id>`.
  - (4) Then the agent runs `seal-check` (it must be all green, `dev_no_passwordless_root` included), gate 4 twice (`--declare eval/z1b/declarations/closure-baseline.json`) and gate 5 (`--commit c753dbf…`).

### 2026-10-20c (Australia/Sydney) — Rust MCP: hybrid p95 target met (175 to 92 ms) with a partitioned FTS5 pool; results identical
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** the last closure target not met (hybrid p95 < 150 ms), on branch `mcp-hybrid`. No ranking change.
- **Profile (live store):** embedding 7 ms, vector scan 16 ms, FTS5 stage 111 / 142 ms p50 / p95 — the OR of the query's words matches ~170k of 303k chunks, and min-max normalisation needs every match, so no LIMIT is possible.
- **From the 2026 docs:** sqlite.org/fts5.html (xRowCount and xColumnTotalSize are whole-table; xQueryPhrase runs `MATCH $p ORDER BY rowid` with no outer constraint; the doclist index serves `rowid BETWEEN` efficiently), ext/fts5/fts5_aux.c (bm25: IDF per phrase from xQueryPhrase, avgdl from whole-table totals, score summed in phrase order, `-1.0 * score`), sqlite.org/mmap.html, sqlite.org/changes.html (3.54.1 of 2026-10-16 is ~4% faster overall, nothing FTS-specific; not taken).
- **Probes before code (all exact checks bit-for-bit, query text never printed):** rowid-range split 1/2/3/4 ways with mmap on persistent connections — FTS stage 87/134 → 49/75 → 37/58 → **30/46 ms**, 0 differing scores over 10.6M rows on all 70 queries (instrument by index + ad hoc). Rejected: per-term split (1–3 ulp differences from clang's fused multiply-add in bm25, and slower at p95), larger page cache or mmap alone (~5%), ranges without mmap (slower beyond 2).
- **Change:** `search::FtsPool` — four persistent read-only connections pinned to one index generation (`open_at`), `PRAGMA mmap_size` (safe: a published index is never written in place), open-ended outer bounds so every rowid lands in a range, queried in parallel and normalised exactly as before; reopened on promote; its first connection also hydrates the hits (no per-call open). Tests: `partitioned_fts_scores_equal_the_single_query_bit_for_bit` (parts 1/2/3/4/7, repeated terms, no-word query, no match), `an_fts_pool_is_never_opened_on_a_promoted_index`, `the_fts_pool_follows_the_index_generation`; the existing promote-storm test still guards generation mixing. The bit-equality tests are guards (they would pass on the old single query); the RED for this change is the measured target.
- **Measured over stdio (same 70 queries x 3, old vs new binary, same session):** hybrid **128 / 175 → 70 / 92 ms** p50 / p95, result text identical on all 70; context ~127 ms and orient ~106 ms median (under parallel load); footprint 164 MB after 100 searches (was 137 MB; the added mappings are clean, file-backed).
- **Live parity gate:** 314 cases per protocol, 312 PASS / 2 EXPECTED_DIVERGENCE (eval prefixes) / 0 FAIL on both 2025-11-25 and 2026-07-28; all 70 hybrid cases identical to Python; instrument identical A = B (hybrid top-1 0.72 / top-3 0.74 / MRR 0.7372).
- **Files:** `rust/zurvan-mcp/src/search.rs`, the design doc (§10.2 note, §10.4 hybrid row), `docs/mcp/rust-server.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/agents-history.md` (rotation).
- **Verification:** cargo test 105 lib + 15 integration passed, clippy `-D warnings` clean; full pytest in this worktree 1529 passed / 17 failed / 1 error, the 17 identical to the base commit's environmental set and the error the worktree-only live-wiki guard (a generated wiki index in the empty worktree wiki); the merged result is checked in the main checkout; `public_repo_guard.py` passed; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Follow-ups:** none; every §1 latency target is now met.

### 2026-10-20b (Australia/Sydney) — Rust MCP: IOU-12 and IOU-13 paid (first status 6.5 s to 74 ms; keyword search 618/1,224 ms to 80/110 ms, results unchanged)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** the two performance IOUs left open by the 2026-10-19 closure, on branch `mcp-perf`, test-first. No ranking change: keyword search keeps its exact substring semantics.
- **IOU-12, persisted index metadata:** `MetaCache::index` writes the served index's per-generation metadata to `data/cache/meta-<key>.json` (0600, 0700 dir, temp + rename, newest four kept; key = index path + dev/ino/size/mtime) and a fresh process reads it instead of scanning `chunks`. A corrupt or mismatched file is recomputed, never trusted; `index_at` on another path never persists. Measured: first `zurvan_status` 5.2 s (compute + persist), then **74 ms** in the next fresh process (target < 100 ms). Fixture fix: three cache-fallback tests now lock `data/` before reading metadata, which otherwise creates `data/cache/` first.
- **IOU-13, keyword search:** profiled first (11,955 files, 212 MB): matching was ~24 ms; the cost was per-file gate canonicalisation (~800 ms serial) and reading plus lowercasing every file. New `src/keyword.rs`: the gate is decided from the walk's already-resolved directory plus the stored leaf name (`security::allowed_canonical`; a symlinked leaf is canonicalised on its own), and the lowercased text lives in a memory-mapped `data/cache/keyword-<key>.zkw` checked per file by identity, with changed files read fresh into an overlay and a rebuild after 256 misses. Every query re-walks and re-gates. Built by the start-up warm-up. Measured warm **p50 80 ms / p95 110 ms** (target < 400 ms), first query in a fresh process 221 ms. Tests (7): equality with the uncached scan cold and warm across case/Unicode/CRLF/binary/traces/symlinks/retrieval reports; edits, new and deleted files seen at once; a symlink swap into `traces/` refused; cache 0600 in 0700 and reused by a fresh process; corrupt cache rebuilt; bulk change rebuilds once; no writable place = the uncached scan. RED shown by routing the cache to the uncached scan (5 of 7 failed; the two guards passed by design).
- **Commit security review (automated) finding, fixed test-first:** the keyword cache's READ path opened a cache file from the shared temp fallback without the private-dir check the write path applies, so another account could plant one and inject text into keyword results. A fallback dir must now pass `vectors::private_dir` before anything in it is read, and a cache file is opened with `O_NOFOLLOW` and trusted only as a regular file owned by this uid with no group/other write bit (`a_planted_cache_in_a_shared_fallback_dir_is_not_trusted`, `a_symlinked_or_shared_writable_cache_file_is_not_trusted`; the first proven to fail with the filter disabled).
- **Live parity gate (keyword results checked against Python text for text):** 314 cases per protocol, 312 PASS / 2 EXPECTED_DIVERGENCE (eval prefixes) / 0 FAIL on both 2025-11-25 and 2026-07-28 (all 70 search_keyword cases identical to Python); instrument identical A = B, hybrid top-1 0.72 / top-3 0.74 / MRR 0.7372, keyword top-1 0.00 / top-3 0.00 / MRR 0.0022 (unchanged).
- **Files:** `rust/zurvan-mcp/src/{keyword.rs (new),corpus.rs,security.rs,search.rs,server.rs,store.rs,vectors.rs,lib.rs}`, the design doc (IOU-12/13 paid, §10.4 rows), `docs/mcp/rust-server.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/agents-history.md` (rotation).
- **Verification:** cargo test 102 lib + 15 integration passed, clippy `-D warnings` clean; full pytest in this worktree 1529 passed / 17 failed / 1 error, the 17 identical to the base commit's environmental set and the 1 error the worktree-only live-wiki guard (a generated wiki index written into the worktree's empty wiki, also present at the base commit); the merged result is checked in the main checkout; `public_repo_guard.py` passed; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Follow-ups:** none owed by this entry. Hybrid p95 (~190 ms against 150 ms) is the one closure target still not met.

**Raouf:**

Before/after on the instrument through the parity harness, keyword group: before top-1 0.00 / top-3 0.00 / MRR 0.0022 (2026-10-20 07:52, both servers); after top-1 0.00 / top-3 0.00 / MRR 0.0022 on both servers, unchanged. The `zurvan` skill (both copies, identical) gained §0a, the MCP server for agents, and a rewritten §12a.

### 2026-10-19b (Australia/Sydney) — Rust MCP server implemented on branch `rust-mcp` (plan Tasks 1–14); live parity gate green on both protocols; cut-over deferred
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** `rust/zurvan-mcp`, a native stdio MCP server serving the same 15 tools, 7 resources and 4 prompts as `scripts/mcp_server.py`; writes and eval stay in Python through `scripts/mcp_bridge.py`. 17 commits on `rust-mcp` from `9820366`, executed inline from `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-rust-mcp-server.md`. Nothing merged, nothing pushed; the harness configs still launch the Python server.
- **Reads (Rust):** search (hybrid and keyword), context, orient, read_page, status, the three graph tools, resources, prompts. Bundled SQLite opened read-only; candle BERT for all-MiniLM-L6-v2 (truncation forced to 256, no padding, overriding `tokenizer.json`); a memory-mapped `.zvec` vector cache (0700 dir, 0600 file, temp + fsync + rename, newest two kept) keyed on the index file's identity; hot reload on index promotion without mixing generations.
- **Writes (Python bridge):** one process per call, no operation of its own, so every write is still exactly one ledger mutation attributed `writer=mcp`, caller `mcp:tool_zurvan_*`. Review fix `86c3854`: the bridge ran in the code root and stamped `project: "zurvan"` where Python stamps `"unknown"`; it now runs in the server's working directory (parity case `writes:decision_add_no_project_foreign_cwd`).
- **Read allow-list (design §5):** `wiki/` except `wiki/traces/`, `docs/`, `eval/`, and the four root docs; `raw/` only with the flag. Judged on the names the filesystem stores, and readers open only the canonical path. Four bypasses found in review and each reproduced as a failing test first: Unicode case folding (`wiki/trace\u017f`), `..` after a missing component, dangling symlinks, and the keyword walk following symlinks out of the root. The Python server is unchanged and still serves `.git/`, `.claude/`, `sources/` (IOU-5, own session proposed).
- **Agent readiness:** server instructions on initialize (the Python server now sends the same text); `isError: true` on error text; a revision stamp on search, context and orient; progress notifications only for a `progressToken`; `zurvan-mcp doctor` and `self-test`; `zurvan_orient` in both servers (IOU-7 paid, +1,053 schema characters).
- **Install (Task 13):** `install_mcp_config.py --engine rust` and `doctor_mcp.py --engine rust`; `docs/mcp/rust-server.md` (build, install, verify, roll back); claude-code and codex docs gain a Rust section.
- **Live parity gate (`scripts/mcp_parity.py`, every group, index rev 745):** 312 cases per protocol, 302 PASS, 10 EXPECTED_DIVERGENCE (8 read-gate probes, 2 eval prefixes), 0 FAIL, on both 2025-11-25 and 2026-07-28. Instrument metrics identical A = B: top-1 0.72, top-3 0.74, MRR 0.7372. The coherent snapshot refused `SNAPSHOT_NOT_READY` (index rev 745 behind the knowledge revision); ruling: do not rebuild the shared index for this measurement, so the evidence is the A/B run on identical bytes and there is no snapshot id.
- **Latency, Rust p50 / p95:** hybrid 129 / 194 ms (Python 11.8 s / 28.0 s); context 174 / 283 ms; orient 138 / 208 ms; keyword 618 / 1,224 ms; status 7.6 / 62 ms warm, but **6.5 s on the first call** of a fresh process. **Not met:** hybrid p95 < 150 ms, keyword < 400 ms (IOU-13), first-call status < 100 ms (IOU-12). Memory after 100 searches: 137.4 MB physical footprint plus a clean 464.8 MiB file-backed map.
- **Final review fix pass (fresh Sonnet reviewer, whole branch):** 0 Critical, 4 Important, 10 Minor (deferred). Fixed test-first, each reproduced RED first: (I1) `zurvan_claim_add`'s `source` now held to the read allow-list before the bridge spawns, so a claim can no longer copy `.git/config` or `sources/` into a readable claim page or act as a guess oracle (`claim_add_source_is_allow_listed`); (I2) `store::open_at` proves each connection is the generation its metadata and vector cache describe, and a call a promote lands in restarts (`open_at_refuses_a_promoted_generation`, `hybrid_never_mixes_generations_under_promotion`: 62 of 248 mixed before, 0 after; the reviewer's stdio storm 1,866 mixed before, 0 after); (I3) an unwritable `data/cache/` falls back to a private 0700 temp-dir cache and the failure names both paths and `zurvan-mcp build-cache` (`unwritable_cache_dir_falls_back_to_a_private_temp_cache`, `cache_failure_names_the_path_and_the_fix`); (I4) the fixed `wiki/` and `eval/` resources open only allow-listed canonical files (`static_resources_open_only_allow_listed_canonical_files`). §9.3's coherent-snapshot check is recorded as UNVERIFIED for this closure.
- **Post-fix end-to-end re-run (2026-10-20, `8ea25bf`, live index rev 785):** parity gate 312 cases per protocol, 302 PASS / 10 EXPECTED_DIVERGENCE / 0 FAIL on both protocols, instrument A = B (top-1 0.72, top-3 0.74, MRR 0.7372); cargo test 94 passed plus the ignored embedding parity test run explicitly; doctor PASS (1 WARN, 5 files newer than the index); self-test 7/7; e2e smoke green on both servers, 0 JSON-RPC parse failures, live wiki untouched; full pytest 17 failed / 1475 passed, the same environmental set as the base commit.
- **Python defects found, not fixed:** IOU-10 `graph_query.get_stats` creates an empty `data/graph.sqlite`; IOU-11 `tool_zurvan_validate_gold` passes `None` and errors on every call; `set_project` resolves a relative store path against the working directory.
- **Files:** `rust/zurvan-mcp/**` (new), `scripts/mcp_bridge.py`, `scripts/mcp_parity.py` (new), `scripts/mcp_server.py`, `scripts/mcp_tools.py`, `scripts/install_mcp_config.py`, `scripts/doctor_mcp.py`, `scripts/e2e_mcp_smoke.py`, `tests/test_mcp_bridge.py`, `tests/test_mcp_rust_parity.py`, `tests/_parity_plant_server.py`, `tests/test_install_mcp_config.py`, `tests/test_doctor_mcp.py`, `docs/mcp/rust-server.md` (new), `docs/mcp/claude-code.md`, `docs/mcp/codex.md`, the design doc (status, §10.1–10.4, IOU-7/8 paid, IOU-10–13), `AGENTS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/agents-history.md` (rotation).

### 2026-10-19 (Australia/Sydney) — Rust MCP server: design approved (rev 3), implementation plan written, phase 0 spike GO
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** design + plan + throwaway spike for replacing `scripts/mcp_server.py` with a Rust `zurvan-mcp` stdio binary. Docs only in the repo; no production code, no index or ledger change. Spike code stayed in the session scratchpad and was not committed.
- **Design:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-rust-mcp-server-design.md`, approved by Raouf at rev 3 (11:07) with the five open questions delegated to the recommendations (§12.1: per-call Python subprocess for writes; default-with-rollback cut-over; FTS5 keyword mode out; read allow-list with no extra paths; `zurvan_orient` in as phase 7). Gauntleted twice: a builder check (7 questions folded in — Python `expand_graph_context` is nondeterministic per `PYTHONHASHSEED`, eval tools measure the Python retriever, near-tie tolerance, instrument choice, no network crates, `{result}` output schemas, bridge must not open its own operation) and a simurgh-arise pass (baseline corrected over real stdio, MCP 2026-07-28 dual lifecycle, per-result revision stamp, agent-readiness contract A1–A12, scorecard, IOU-1…9).
- **Measured baseline (Python server over stdio, 10:51):** initialize 0.66 s; first hybrid search 26.8 s; warm 6.8 s; `zurvan_status` 11.9 s (`MAX(indexed_at)` is a 5.5 s full scan); negotiated protocol 2025-11-25 with `ttlMs: 0`; tool schemas ~4.2k tokens/session.
- **Security finding (not fixed here):** `zurvan_read_page` returns `.git/`, `.claude/settings.json`, `.remember/now.md` and ingested legal documents under `sources/`; the gate blocks only absolute paths, traversal and `raw/`. Design §5 replaces it with an allow-list in Rust; the Python fix (IOU-5) is proposed as its own session. Private note written via `zurvan remember`.
- **Plan:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-rust-mcp-server.md` — 15 tasks (0 spike, 1–13 build to cut-over, 14 `zurvan_orient`), live A/B parity harness instead of stored goldens, Review Focus on CRLF/code-point slicing, punctuation-only queries, mid-session index promotion, missing model, case-variant paths.
- **Spike verdict — GO:** on index rev 745 (303,080 chunks), 50 instrument items by index + 20 ad-hoc queries: hybrid top-10 identical to Python **70/70**; candle-vs-sentence-transformers query cosine min 1.000000; max score diff 1.91e-7; warm Rust hybrid **139 ms p50 / 200 ms p95** vs Python 6.8 s quiet / 11.9 s same-session; matrix build 6.1 s cold; no network crates; 7.5 MB binary. p95 misses the 150 ms target (FTS-bound) and is reported as such.
- **Spike finding that changed the plan:** selecting `chunk_id` from the external-content `chunks_fts` costs one `chunks` lookup per matching row (4.79 s cold / 0.37 s warm on one query); selecting `rowid` returns the identical `(chunk_id, bm25)` set at 0.18–0.20 s. The vector cache stores `chunks.rowid` per row; Python's 5.3 s p50 FTS stage has the same cause (IOU-9, needs its own before/after eval).
- **Files:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-rust-mcp-server-design.md` (new), `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-rust-mcp-server.md` (new), `AGENTS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/agents-history.md` (rotation).
- **Verification:** `git diff --check` clean; `public_repo_guard.py` passed; both docs checked programmatically for instrument query text (none). No retrieval ranking or index change, so no `eval_search` re-run is owed by this entry.
- **Follow-ups:** plan Task 1 onward (execution method to be chosen); Python read-gate session (IOU-5); IOU-9 Python FTS rowid; IOU-4 Python expansion determinism.

### 2026-10-06 (Australia/Sydney) — Z1b Rev 3 #1: operator closure — DEV frozen, suite authored+sealed, real VM path made deployable, leak-scan incident; fresh suite drafting
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** operator closure steps 4–7 on the real machine (operator-authorised; sudo via stdin, never stored), run from the `z1b-rev3` worktree. 15 commits on `z1b-rev3` (`57808b6`…`04bee6f`), each test-first + mutation-checked + re-installed; every defect below was found by RUNNING the real path, not reading. Nothing merged, nothing pushed. **#1 / Z1b NOT CLOSED.**
- **DEV set frozen (`57808b6`):** 203 items (12–13/stratum) authored by four fresh Sonnet agents against the frozen corpus, independently verified (schema, targets in index, headings, leakage, dups, verbatim grounding); `dev_validate` ok; digest `36c69543…`.
- **Author login (runbook was wrong):** models run as `zurvanauthor` (Rev 3 #2), not `zurvaneval`; creds must live in `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=/Users/zurvanauthor/private/claude`; `sudo -i` fails (shell `/usr/bin/false`). macOS Claude needs a keychain: a hidden user's keychain may NOT be named `login` (bound to the account password `*`), and unlock state is per process tree. Fix: dedicated `zurvan-author.keychain-db` (0600, no auto-lock), random password root-only at `/private/var/root/zurvan-author-keychain.pw`, unlocked in the same process tree that runs `claude`.
- **Drafting defects:** one DNS blip killed a 2 h run → bounded call retry (`531cd98`); runs came back SHORT twice (RESERVE 79/80, then `hash` 0/15 from ONE malformed author JSON reply + `semantic` 54% yield) → 24 candidates/stratum (`3e65112`), then re-ask + bounded per-stratum top-up (`50f6cab`); seal could not reach the bundle (no traverse on the author home) → search-only ACL (`f8ca598`). Suite `7c18c2e16c0a3119` sealed: HOLDOUT 160 / RESERVE 80, seal-check **23/23**.
- **The installed broker could never execute a candidate — found by a FREE rehearsal before spending budget (`cdf5cd2`):** limactl/Lima assets never installed; VM config pinned the Alpine digest (Lima aborted), 1 GiB, 4 GiB disk; LIMA_HOME unwritable to the VM identity; pf blocked loopback; guest login user root-equivalent + DNS resolving via the host → candidate egress. Fix: VM runs AS `zurvancandidate` through the existing digest-pinned door (`vm-*` verbs; still 2 sudoers rules; door renders the config itself); golden profile; candidate runs in-guest as no-sudo `z1bcand` with iptables/ip6tables owner-REJECT on all non-loopback.
- **Security review findings, reproduced then fixed:** shared candidate-owned LIMA_HOME (`_config/override.yaml` poisons later runs) → fresh per-run lima home (`a7b9919`); loopback exemption reached host services (mysqld, cloudflared) + DNS via mDNSResponder → pf allows only a reserved ssh range (40000–40999, below ephemeral) + `GODEBUG=netdns=go` (`a7b9919`, `4c098cf`); reply rule `flags any` let a reserved-port SYN reach mysqld (reproduced: BYPASS-OPEN) → `flags A/A no state` (`197977c`, `a90a3c0`). Verified live as guest ROOT and as `z1bcand`: internet TCP, host loopback services and DNS all blocked.
- **The VM ran the image's BAKED candidate, not the evaluated commit (`174171b`):** door verb `vm-push-tree` builds `git archive <commit>` from the verified bundle AS the candidate; guest runs `/opt/z1b/cand/scripts/z1b_candidate.py`. Free rehearsal through the real door: boot + 1.3 GB corpus in ~115 s, the commit's candidate answers over the protocol, all egress blocked, clean teardown.
- **Closure runs:** baseline predeclaration committed (`981f656`); gate 4 dirty-tree check PASSED (`CANDIDATE_NOT_FROZEN`, uncharged). First charged attempt refused uncharged `SCAN_INCOMPLETE`: scanner must LIST `~/.claude/projects` (setup granted search only) → `list,search,readattr` no inheritance (`acba53b`; security review flagged name disclosure — **kept, Raouf's decision**: names only, contents unreadable, scan stays fail-closed on new dirs).
- **Leak-scan incident `438dfd7ca96ba06a`:** `item_leak`, 8 sealed items burned (irreversible) and resealed → HOLDOUT 158 / RESERVE 74 → **gate 8 FAILED for suite `7c18c2e16c0a3119`** (recorded, not redefined). Diagnosed as `zurvaneval`, aggregates only: most likely sealed queries quoting ≥64 informative corpus bytes (e.g. sha256 digests) whose pages DEV-authoring agents had read — not a real leak, correctly fail-closed. Fix upstream (`d92c8ee`): authoring rejects corpus-quoting queries with the scanner's OWN matcher (`corpus_quote`); composition test: anything accepted → zero scanner hits. Raouf chose: fix + fresh suite. Old suite archived (not deleted) to `/Users/zurvaneval/retired/`; incident acknowledged by Raouf in a terminal.
- **Process slip, corrected:** `d92c8ee` was committed with one failing test (broken by `acba53b`); fix-forward `04bee6f`. Correction carried from 2026-10-04: seal-check has 23 checks.
- **Verification:** `-k z1b` 1684 passed / 4 skipped / 1 failed (`gate2`: `sudo -n` cannot reach cached creds from pytest — property verified directly against the real sealed files, not counted as passing). Live seal gates otherwise green. Budget still **8** (no charged run yet).
- **Now / remaining:** fresh suite drafting on `04bee6f` (started 07:34). Then seal → seal-check 23/23 → clean scan → gate 4 (run twice, cached) → gate 5 (escape probe) → tamper drill → gate 9 red-team → verdict → ff-only merge. No automatic push.

### 2026-10-03 (Australia/Sydney) — Z1b Rev 3 #1: corpus provisioning reworked to IN-GUEST build (host supplies content only)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** code on `z1b-rev3` (`762790a`): `scripts/eval_seal/broker.py`, `scripts/eval_seal/vm_runner.py`, tests `test_z1b_broker_vm.py`, `test_z1b_vm.py`, `test_z1b_vm_live.py`, `test_z1b_broker.py`. Status/design on `main` (§7k.1 implementation). Branch unmerged; nothing pushed.
- **Invariant now in code (§7k.1):** the HOST supplies corpus CONTENT only; the GUEST constructs the immutable read-only filesystem. The prior host-built `corpus.squashfs` staged-and-mounted model is removed — macOS has no system tool to build a Linux-mountable read-only fs (mksquashfs is brew-only, forbidden as a privileged-command head) and a packaging artifact inside `corpus/` would corrupt `corpus_sha256(corpus_dir)`. Supersedes the earlier "stage + mount a host squashfs" note in the rotated 2026-10-02 and operator-install entries.
- **`broker_provision_corpus(run_id, paths, corpus_sha)`:** tars the frozen corpus CONTENT (deterministic: sorted, root-owned, mtime 0) to a 0600 file in the eval runs dir; `limactl copy`s the TAR in (scp over lima, never a host mount/image); then one `sudo /bin/sh -c` IN-GUEST extracts → re-derives `corpus_sha256` of the bytes that ACTUALLY arrived and refuses (exit 3 → CANDIDATE_FAILED) unless it equals the declared identity (a byte tampered after the digest cannot be mounted) → mksquashfs → mount `-o ro` → wipe plaintext+tar+image. Fail-closed; no writable fallback. `corpus_sha` threaded through `launch_candidate`/`broker_vm_launch`. Removed obsolete host-side `verify_corpus_image`/`corpus_image_sha256`. Fixed a real `-c` shell-quoting bug in the verify one-liner (would have broken in-guest too).
- **Tests:** unit prove content-only staging (a TAR, not a built image), source corpus byte-identical afterwards with no artifact added, malformed-sha refusal before staging, and the KEY tamper-after-digest refusal by running the broker's OWN guest script on the host (guest-path tokens rewritten, mksquashfs/mount stubbed) with a passing untampered control. Live gate (`ZURVAN_Z1B_VM_LIVE=1`) reworked to drive the real path against the GOLDEN image (bakes squashfs-tools; G2/G8 PASS): read-only under candidate sudo, staged bytes wiped, host corpus unchanged, tamper refused, second guest pristine.
- **Verification:** `-k z1b` **1638 passed / 2 skipped**; `public_repo_guard` + `boundary scan-diff` + `git diff --check` clean. Last planned code pass for #1; remaining is operator-only (`install_corpus` content + sealed-suite authoring + `zurvan eval z1b run` installed-broker gate, Raouf-only).

### 2026-10-02 (Australia/Sydney) — Z1b Rev 3 #1: read-only corpus provisioning PROVEN on real VZ (developer closure of #1 complete)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** `scripts/eval_seal/vm_runner.py`, `tests/test_z1b_vm.py`, `tests/test_z1b_vm_live.py` on branch `z1b-rev3` (`18612aa`). Status on `main`: Rev 3 design §7b/§7f/§7g + progress doc, this CHANGELOG. No production code on `main`; no sudo, identity creation, authoring or model spend.
- **What this proves.** The third developer-level property of #1 (VM-per-run candidate isolation): the candidate READS the frozen eval corpus but can never mutate it, and the exact corpus is bound to the run identity. Completes the developer trio with VM isolation (`c3ddb28`) and broker-only execution (`1adeb18`).
- **Mechanism + honest refinement.** §7f(a) specified a `readonly` virtio disk; the gate uses a **squashfs** staged by `limactl copy` (scp over lima's ssh, NOT a host mount), loop-mounted `-o ro`. Stronger than a flag — squashfs has no kernel write path, so a compromised root in the guest cannot `remount,rw`. `verify_corpus_image` fails closed (`CORPUS_MISMATCH`) unless the image is a squashfs (`hsqs` magic) whose whole-file sha256 equals the declared identity (immutability and correct-identity are separate checks). `vm_copy_into_guest` stages it (`CANDIDATE_FAILED` on failure).
- **Live gate (opt-in + serial, `ZURVAN_Z1B_VM_LIVE=1`).** Clean host: read the known file; write/create/delete/rename all fail "Read-only file system" even under sudo; no `/Users` mount; host image byte-unchanged; second guest pristine; teardown leaves no instance; wrong-digest rejection asserted. Positive control: the same write-probe on a writable tmpfs SUCCEEDS, so the read-only assertions are load-bearing.
- **Operational finding (recorded, not a defect).** An interrupted run orphans a `limactl hostagent` VZ process that contends for VZ and flakes later back-to-back boots. Production is fail-closed: `vm_start` refuses a leftover instance (`SANDBOX_VIOLATION`). A dev re-run reaps orphans first (`ps | grep ZurvanEval/broker/bin/limactl`).
- **Files:** `scripts/eval_seal/vm_runner.py`, `tests/test_z1b_vm.py`, `tests/test_z1b_vm_live.py` (branch `z1b-rev3`); `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-02-z1b-rev3-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/progress/2026-10-02-z1b-plan-gauntlet.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `docs/agents-history.md` (on `main`).
- **Verification:** full live module **3 passed in 237 s** on a clean host; corpus gate + positive control **1 passed in 121 s**; 53 fake-runner unit + 5 broker-vm wiring tests; `-k z1b` **1593 passed / 2 skipped**; py3.9 parse clean; `public_repo_guard` + `boundary scan-diff` clean; `git diff --check` clean.
- **#1 is NOT CLOSED.** Operator closure remains (§7f): golden retrieval image + the installed-broker closure gate (`tests/test_z1b_seal_live.py`, needs `sudo setup.sh`/`install.sh`, Raouf-only). Next: P1 queue #7 → #13 → #16 → #14 → #12.

### 2026-10-02 (Australia/Sydney) — Z1b gauntlet round 2: all findings fixed; main index-rebuild test repaired
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** fixed every finding the inline Simurgh gauntlet raised, test-first with mutation checks. Dry-run code on branch `z1b-gauntlet`; the plan on `main` records the fixes. No production Z1b on `main` still; no sudo, authoring or model spend.
- **Fixed:**
  - **G20 (HIGH):** only a public projection of a run result crosses the seal — no per-stratum block, no bootstrap intervals, latency/RSS snapped up a coarse ladder; the full result stays in the sealed ledger; the dev client refuses anything that is not a projection. Covert-channel residual drops from ~376 to <= ~56 bits per declared run (measured by exhaustive enumeration). `scripts/eval_seal/output.py`, `broker.py`, `scripts/z1b_client.py`; `tests/test_z1b_public_output.py` (21 tests, 4 guards mutation-checked).
  - **G19 (LOW):** three stdlib-only tests used `sys.stdlib_module_names` (3.10+) and silently skipped on the 3.9 sealed interpreter; they now resolve against the running interpreter's `sysconfig` stdlib dir and run on 3.9.

### 2026-09-28d (Australia/Sydney) — Z0 step 5: rebuilds read a snapshot proven to be revision R, stamp it, and report CURRENT, STALE or INVALID
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** new `scripts/build_snapshot.py` (capture proven by double manifest, drift minted at capture, SUSPECT refused, waits for live operations), new `scripts/derived_health.py`, `scripts/measurement_snapshot.py` (`docs`/`roots` components, `BUILD_INPUTS`), `scripts/rebuild_search_index.py` (`snapshot=`, `index_meta` stamps, snapshot-backed `__main__`), `scripts/graph_build.py` (`source_root`, stamps in `graph_meta`, snapshot-backed `__main__`), `scripts/cli.py` (`knowledge status` health lines), new `tests/test_build_snapshot.py` (13) and `tests/test_derived_health.py` (7), 2 tests in `test_snapshot_components.py`, two step-4 fakes updated. Design §10.3 status, amendments spec §10 (amendment 8), `CLAUDE.md`. Plan rev 2 (two gauntlets). Branch `z0-step5-snapshot-rebuilds`.
- **What it closes.** Z0 gate items 17 (truthful stamp), 18 (SUSPECT refusal), 12 at capture, 11 (capture race retries, never a mixed state) and the second half of 5 (reconcile, then rebuild → CURRENT), each by a named test. Item 9 is PARTIAL (measurement-capture coherence is step 6). **Z0 stays OPEN.**
- **Four design amendments, each forced by a measurement** (amendment 8): a wiki-only snapshot would have dropped `docs/` and the root files (~1% of chunks — a ranking change), so build inputs are captured; a clone's new inodes make a snapshot manifest never match, so coherence is proven on the live side; snapshots are released after the build (retention is step 6); a store with no ledger builds unstamped and reports INVALID.
- **Two gauntlets before execution caught six defects by running the plan**, including a capture that gave up in 0.64 s while another process held an operation for 2 s (blaming the store for "changing" — a rebuild during any ingest would have failed), and a `_collect_graph(root=...)` parameter that its own `os.walk` loop variable would have shadowed after the first directory.
- **Rulings during execution.** (1) `derived_health` resolves the search index from `ZURVAN_SEARCH_DB` or the store root resolved at call time — NOT `config.get_search_db_path()`, which the plan named: its `PROJECT_ROOT` binds at import, so an explicit root saw the LIVE index (caught by `test_a_missing_store_is_invalid`). (2) Only two step-4 fakes needed new signatures, not three.
- **Ranking-neutral, proven on frozen inputs.** A clonefile-frozen corpus with its own ledger: the snapshot-backed build and the old live-reading build give identical graph content (`9524c2b9…`, 4,694 / 3,948) and identical search chunk sets (`8aecb526…`, 187,042). The retriever never reads `index_meta`. Live eval after the gate: Top-1 72% / Top-3 74% on chunk-set `8aecb52602c6`.
- **Falsified, all behavioural.** Removing the after-clone manifest check, the SUSPECT refusal, the snapshot read, or the wait each turns its named tests red (1 / 1 / 1 / 3). SUSPECT is defended twice: with this refusal removed, `observe_external_drift`'s own guard still refuses — the test catches the change of error type.
- **Live.** The gate's rebuilds ran through the new path: the live index stamped from revision 223, the graph from 227; `knowledge status` then read `search: COHERENT+STALE (built from 223) — 4 revision(s) behind`, `graph: COHERENT+CURRENT` — §6.2's own example, on the real store. No rebuild snapshot left behind.
- **Observed, not caused here.** Every `check.sh` run ingests and extracts its sample documents into the LIVE store: the ingests end ABORTED ("changed nothing"), but each extract rewrites its pages and commits a revision (5 per gate), so derived stores are STALE the moment the gate finishes. Before 2026-09-28 those rewrites were unrecorded or laundered; now they are attributed.
- **Verification.** `check.sh` **EXIT=0**, pytest **1270 passed / 4 skipped / 0 failed** (1248 + 13 + 7 + 2), red-team **27/27**. The independent `wiki/*.md` hash showed `wiki/log.md` changed during the gate: another project's MCP/CLI write-backs at 13:32–13:35, no test-session token — external, correctly classified.
- **Final review (fresh reviewer, Opus): no Critical, three Important, all fixed RED→GREEN, plus one minor re-graded in.** (1) `knowledge status` crashed on a graph built before `graph_meta` existed, on a non-numeric stamp and on a corrupt file; health now reports INVALID ("unreadable") instead, and at the current revision also requires the stamp's manifest to be the ledger's. (2) `test_gate_5_reconcile_then_rebuild_is_current` never reconciled — the defect was the plan's; it now SIGKILLs a writer, reconciles, then rebuilds, and goes red when `reconcile` is made a no-op.
