- **Scope:** `scripts/public_repo_guard.py`, its red-team suite, and the two log files. Found while auditing the pending push before sending it — a pre-push review that was itself the control that caught this. No retrieval/ranking change.
- **The defect.** `PRIVATE_PROJECT_TAGS` was a hand-written literal **inside tracked source on a public remote**. The whole purpose of that list is to name projects that must never be published; spelling them in a committed file publishes them directly. The pending push carried the names in **4 files and 25 added lines** — 5 in the guard, 13 in `tests/test_repo_guard_redteam.py` (used as fixture literals), 3 in `CHANGELOG.md` and 4 in `docs/agents-history.md` (prose that enumerated the private projects to explain why the denylist failed open). Among them a **person's name** and a **tenancy project**. Nothing had been pushed yet, so nothing leaked.
- **Fix.** The names now live in `.private-projects`, gitignored, one lowercase tag per line with `#` comments; `_load_private_project_tags()` reads it and **returns an empty frozenset when the file is absent**. An empty veto is safe by construction: since 13e, publishability requires a `project:` field naming a project in `PUBLIC_PROJECTS`, so the veto is defence in depth and never the thing deciding. A fresh clone therefore gets a guard that is strictly no weaker, without shipping the list.
- **Tests must not carry the data either.** The red-team fixtures now use invented names (`acme-private`, `other-private`) with an autouse fixture patching `PRIVATE_PROJECT_TAGS`, so the suite tests the *mechanism* rather than the current contents of a private list — which also means the tests no longer break when a name is added or removed.
- **Log prose scrubbed** in both `CHANGELOG.md` and `docs/agents-history.md`: the 13d/13e entries now describe the failure mode ("an unlisted private project", "a private research corpus") without enumerating. The engineering reasoning is unchanged; only the identifying nouns are gone.
- **History rewritten before pushing.** Scrubbing the working tree is not enough: the names sat in blobs of the five unpushed commits, so `git show <sha>` on the remote would have served them. The range `origin/main..HEAD` was rewritten with a whole-word substitution (word boundaries, longest-first) applied to every commit, so no blob at any pushed commit contains a private name. `origin/main` was verified clean beforehand — the leak existed only in local history.
- **Also in this commit:** `scripts/zurvan` picks an interpreter that can `import yaml` (bare `python3` is a PEP 668 Homebrew 3.14 without PyYAML on this machine, so `zurvan <cmd>` died with `ModuleNotFoundError`), honouring `ZURVAN_PYTHON`; covered by `tests/test_cli.py::test_zurvan_wrapper_honors_python_override`.
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/public_repo_guard.py` (+`_load_private_project_tags`, `PRIVATE_PROJECTS_FILE`; literal list removed), `.gitignore` (+`.private-projects`), `tests/test_repo_guard_redteam.py` (invented fixtures + autouse patch), `scripts/zurvan`, `tests/test_cli.py` (+1), `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/agents-history.md`, AGENTS.md.
- **Verification:** `pytest` -> **603 passed, 0 failed**. `public_repo_guard.py` passed. `git diff --check` clean. Private-name scan over the whole pending push (all commits, all added lines, and the final tree) -> **0 matches**; same scan over `origin/main` -> 0. Guard behaviour re-checked with `.private-projects` absent: still blocks every undeclared page.
- **Follow-ups:** (1) The veto list is now local-only, so a second machine has no veto until the file is created there — acceptable (the allowlist decides) but worth a line in the README if anyone else ever clones this. (2) Nothing automatically checks a diff for private names before commit; that scan was manual here and would be a reasonable `check.sh` gate, though it would need the same gitignored list to read from. (3) `wiki/open-questions.md` still holds 91 lines of uncommitted private cross-project content and stays unstaged, as in 13e.

### 2026-09-13h (Australia/Sydney) — Retrieval: 50-query gold set, corrected fusion weight, and the index-poisoning bug behind it all
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Retrieval evaluation and ranking. Per invariant 4 the before/after `eval_search` runs are recorded below. Also fixes a latent bug that had been silently destroying semantic search.
- **1. The eval could not see anything.** `eval/search_gold.jsonl` held **3 queries** and reported top-3 100% / MRR 0.83 — on n=3 that is noise, not signal, and it cannot distinguish a real gain from chance. Grew it to **50 queries**, each written from a distinct tracked document's actual content (never from what the system currently returns, which would be circular) and each `expected_path` validated as a real public file, since `eval/` is committed. On the honest gold set the starting point was **top-1 40%, top-3 62%, MRR 0.52** — the 3-query set had been hiding a third of the failures.
- **2. THE INDEX WAS POISONED, and I caused it.** `scripts/e2e_smoke.sh` exports `ZURVAN_EMBED_PROVIDER=mock` and then rebuilds the index; running the e2e — twice, as asked — replaced all 26,051 real vectors with deterministic hashes. Semantic-only retrieval measured **0%**. Every fusion measurement taken before this was discovered is therefore void. **Fix:** new `config.get_search_db_path()` honours `ZURVAN_SEARCH_DB`, `hybrid_search`/`context_export`/`rebuild_search_index` all resolve through it, and the smoke test redirects the WHOLE run (check.sh rebuilds too, and inherits the environment — a first, narrower fix missed that and the index was poisoned a second time). Verified: provider is `sentence_transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2` both before and after a full e2e.
- **3. The keyword weight was backwards.** With real embeddings restored, keyword-only scores MRR 0.547 and semantic-only 0.651 — the WEAKER retriever held the larger weight (0.6). Measured sweep: w=0.6 -> top1 52% / top3 70% / MRR 0.619; w=0.3 -> 66/72/0.711; **w=0.2 -> 70/78/0.742**; w=0.15 -> 70/78/0.740. 0.15 and 0.2 are a plateau, not a spike, so this is a trend rather than a value overfitted to 50 queries. **Before -> after on the real index: top-1 52% -> 70%, top-3 70% -> 78%, MRR 0.619 -> 0.75, failures 19 -> 11.**
- **4. Things I tried and REJECTED on measurement**, each of which the 2026 guidance recommends: **Reciprocal Rank Fusion** — lost on every metric but top-3 (56/74/0.652), because it weights both retrievers equally by construction and over-promotes the weaker keyword ordering here; fusing untruncated rankings was worse still (34% top-3). **Chunk overlap** — measured exactly neutral (70/78/0.75 unchanged) and it pushed chunks past `MAX_CHUNK_CHARS`, which is sized to the embedder's window, so it was reverted. **A better embedding model** — the docs call `all-MiniLM-L6-v2` (MTEB 56.3) "not competitive for production retrieval", but on this corpus **bge-small-en-v1.5 scored 62/72/0.688** (62/72/0.679 with its recommended query prefix) and **bge-base-en-v1.5 62/68/0.68**, both clearly behind MiniLM's 70/78/0.75. MTEB is a prior, not an oracle; MiniLM stays. Full index rebuilds were done for each so the comparison is real, not extrapolated.
- **5. Two bugs the above surfaced.** `search_hybrid` printed "index not found" to **stdout**, corrupting `--json` output — moved to stderr. And `eval_search`'s quality gate now **skips** when the index holds mock embeddings, stating why: judging semantic retrieval against hash vectors measures nothing, and that gate had only ever "passed" during the smoke test *because* the run had poisoned the real index — it was grading the poisoning.
- **Files Changed:** `eval/search_gold.jsonl` (3 -> 50 queries), `scripts/hybrid_search.py` (`KEYWORD_WEIGHT`, `_fuse`, stderr diagnostic, shared path helper), `scripts/config.py` (+`get_search_db_path`), `scripts/rebuild_search_index.py`, `scripts/context_export.py`, `scripts/eval_search.py` (+`_index_uses_mock_embeddings`, gate skip), `scripts/e2e_smoke.sh` (whole-run index redirect), `tests/test_retrieval_tuning.py` (new, 10), `tests/test_config.py` (+4), `tests/test_eval_search.py` (+3), `tests/test_hybrid_search.py` (2 latent test bugs fixed), AGENTS.md + CHANGELOG.md + docs/agents-history.md.
- **Verification:** `pytest` -> **600 passed, 0 failed**. `eval_search` before/after recorded above. **Full E2E smoke passed (exit 0) with the index provably preserved** across the run, and the retrieval gate correctly skipped twice under mock. `public_repo_guard` passed; `git diff --check` clean; **frozen provenance golds unchanged** (86% / 100% / 0% / 100% / 100%). Snapshot before the work: `dist/snapshots/zurvan_snapshot_20260913_130904.tar.gz`. Committed; **not pushed**.
- **Follow-ups:** (1) **The frozen 86% provenance recall is not a live KPI and cannot move** — it scores committed trace JSON, so retrieval changes never touch it; its 4 misses are `docs/review/overview.md`, `docs/review/citation-review.md`, `docs/mcp/security.md` and `docs/publication/citation-appendix.md`, two of them from the gold set's own "hard / ambiguous" category. Live quality lives in `eval_search`. (2) **Reranking and query expansion remain untried** — both need either a cross-encoder dependency or a real LLM provider, which is a dependency decision rather than a tuning one. Given RRF and two better-benchmarked embedding models both lost here, neither should be adopted without measuring it on this gold set first. (3) 50 queries is enough to see a trend but not to split hairs; the 11 remaining failures are the obvious next thing to read. (4) `MIN_HYBRID_SCORE` is now near-vestigial: with semantic at 0.8 weight every chunk starts around 0.4, so `limit` does the real truncation.

### 2026-09-13g (Australia/Sydney) — Figures: make the chart gap loud, and get the images out
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** `scripts/ingest.py` figure inventory + a new `scripts/export_figures.py`. Addresses the accuracy gap left open by 13f. Test-and-tooling change; no retrieval ranking change.
- **The problem.** A PDF's text layer contains **nothing** of what a chart shows, and nothing warned about it. The intelligence-targeting paper carried 4 raster charts whose per-model values appear nowhere in the prose — pooled GPS medians, the five hardest terminal-guidance settings, the whole GPS arrival-rate panel — and the cybersecurity report carried 41 figures. Worse, the caption-count heuristic that worked on one paper returned **zero** on the other, because it uses no "Figure N" captions: a check that reports "no figures" on a 10-figure document is worse than no check. Both gaps were found only by manually looking.
- **Change.** `pdf_figure_inventory()` counts embedded images per page (fails soft on a broken PDF), and every PDF source page now carries `figures: N` / `figures_transcribed: false` in frontmatter plus, when N > 0, a **"Figures — NOT transcribed"** section naming the pages and stating plainly that the content is absent from the text and that a claim must not be anchored to a figure through the page. The gap is now stated on the artefact itself rather than left to be discovered.
- **Deliberately NOT automated.** `export_figures.py` does the deterministic half only — pull the images, skip page furniture below a size floor, downscale, print an inventory — and calls no model, writes nothing into the wiki. Reading a bar chart is a visual judgement, and a number invented here would be indistinguishable from a real one downstream. That also keeps it clear of invariant 2 (no LLM extraction under the mock provider).
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/ingest.py` (+`pdf_figure_inventory`, `render_figure_notice`; `create_source_page` emits the frontmatter and notice), `scripts/export_figures.py` (new), `tests/test_figures.py` (new, 11 tests), 5 source pages re-ingested, AGENTS.md + CHANGELOG.md + docs/agents-history.md.
- **Verification:** `pytest` -> **586 passed, 0 failed**. Re-ingested pages now declare their figures: cybersecurity 42, risk report 15, intelligence-targeting 10, appendix 1, and the automated-alignment paper correctly **0** (its figures are vector-drawn, so their labels already extract as text). Exporter checked on the real paper: 10 figures out, downscaled 2823x1198 -> 1412x599, and the terminal-guidance chart re-read at 112 KB (was 291 KB) with **every value still legible and matching the earlier hand transcription**. All **248** anchorable claims re-validated, 0 broken. **Full E2E smoke passed** (all 23 check.sh gates) and MCP E2E smoke passed. `public_repo_guard` passed; `git diff --check` clean; **frozen provenance golds unchanged** (86% / 100% / 0% / 100% / 100%). Committed; **not pushed**.
- **Follow-ups:** (1) A vision pass is still manual: export, look, write a note. Worth a `zurvan figures read` command once a real LLM provider is configured, gated so it can never fabricate a value. (2) `figures_transcribed: false` is currently always false — nothing flips it when a companion note is written; a `zurvan figures mark <source>` would close the loop. (3) Vector-drawn figures (the automated-alignment paper) extract their labels as text but not their DATA POINTS; the inventory correctly reports 0 images there, which is honest but does not mean full coverage.

### 2026-09-13f (Australia/Sydney) — PDF ingest switched to PyMuPDF + normalisation (4x faster, ligature- and wrap-clean)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** `scripts/ingest.py` PDF path. Measured against the real corpus before changing anything, then the 5 ingested PDFs re-ingested and every claim re-validated. Retrieval-input change, so an `eval_search` run is documented below per invariant 4.
- **Why.** Today's four ingests exposed two extraction defects that make text keyword-invisible and unquotable. pypdf emitted **191 ligature glyphs** across three Anthropic PDFs (`identiﬁable` is not `identifiable` to BM25, and `claim add` checks evidence byte-for-byte), and one paper arrived with **98% of its lines holding a single word**, which defeats phrase search outright. Both had to be repaired by hand mid-session.
- **Measured, not assumed** (3 PDFs, 93 pages): pypdf 2.88s / 191 ligatures; **PyMuPDF 0.71s / 0 ligatures** — 4x faster; pdfplumber 11.38s, 16x slower, 173 glued words on the LaTeX paper and **silently dropped 20,000 characters**, so it is not a general-purpose upgrade. Whole 107-PDF Resaerch corpus projects to ~22s of parsing.
- **Developer docs checked first**, and they did NOT hold empirically. `TEXT_PRESERVE_LIGATURES` is documented as defaulting on with expansion when cleared, but PyMuPDF's `get_text()` already returned 0 ligatures on these files regardless; and `TEXT_DEHYPHENATE` had **zero effect** on extraction output (39 hyphen-breaks before and after), consistent with its documented same-block-only limitation. So no flags are set; the normalisation pass does the work.
- **Change:** `_extract_pdf()` uses PyMuPDF with a **pypdf fallback** so an environment without PyMuPDF still ingests; `normalize_extracted_text()` expands all 7 ligatures, normalises non-breaking spaces, rejoins wrapped lines and collapses space runs. Deliberately does NOT join hyphens at line ends: the flag does not work, and a naive join corrupts compounds (`state-\nof-the-art` -> `stateof-the-art`) for a measured cost of only 39 breaks per 245k characters.
- **A bug my own test caught.** The first normalisation treated any whitespace-only line as a paragraph break. Measured against the real files that was wrong in the worst way: pypdf output stayed at **97% one-word lines and gained 2,959 false paragraphs**. Only a TRULY empty line is a paragraph break — a `\n \n` is the word separator in mangled output. Fixed and covered by `test_one_word_per_line_wrapping_is_rejoined` and `test_paragraph_breaks_survive_unwrapping`.
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/ingest.py` (+`LIGATURES`, `normalize_extracted_text`, `_extract_pdf`; rewritten `extract_text`), `tests/test_ingest.py` (+9 regression tests), 5 source pages re-ingested, 4 claim quotes re-anchored, AGENTS.md + CHANGELOG.md + docs/agents-history.md.
- **Verification:** `pytest` -> **575 passed, 0 failed**. Re-ingested pages: ligatures **191 -> 0** across the set; one-word lines **98% -> 9%** worst case (others 1-5%). This also clears the standing open question about the Aug-2026 Risk Report, whose page had ~1,160 keyword-invisible ligature words and is now **0**. All **247** anchorable claims re-validated: 4 broke exactly as predicted (all ligature quotes), were re-anchored to the expanded text, and the suite is **0 broken**. **Full E2E smoke test passed** (`scripts/e2e_smoke.sh`, all 23 check.sh gates) and **MCP E2E smoke passed**. `public_repo_guard` passed; `git diff --check` clean. **Frozen provenance golds unchanged**: 86% recall / 100% completeness / 0% raw leak / 100% hash / 100% graph. **eval_search after the change: Top-3 100%, MRR 0.83, 0 failures** against the 0.6 gate (no before-figure was captured this session; the gate threshold is the documented floor). Snapshot taken before any change: `dist/snapshots/zurvan_snapshot_20260913_123702.tar.gz`. Smoke-test residue removed from the tracked `wiki/open-questions.md`. Committed; **not pushed**.
- **Follow-ups:** (1) **No parser recovers chart data.** The intelligence-targeting paper has 4 raster charts whose values appear nowhere in the text, and the cybersecurity report 41 figures; that needs a vision pass, not a better extractor, and remains the largest accuracy gap. (2) Docling and Marker were NOT benchmarked — neither is installed, both pull heavy ML dependencies, and Marker in particular may beat this on the many arXiv/LaTeX papers in the corpus; worth measuring before adopting. (3) LlamaParse was rejected on architecture, not preference: it is a cloud API, `raw/` holds private cross-project documents, and AGENTS.md mandates local-first. (4) 102 of the 107 PDFs in ~/Desktop/Resaerch remain un-ingested; a bulk run is now ~22s of parsing plus embedding time.

### 2026-09-13e (Australia/Sydney) — Close the two residual guard holes: declared-project allowlist + case-folded ignores
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** The two follow-ups left open by 13d, both of which were fail-OPEN paths. Guard/ignore change plus a one-field frontmatter migration; no retrieval/ranking change.
- **Hole 1 — the private-tag denylist was load-bearing and failed open.** A page tagged `zurvan` (public) **plus an unlisted private project** passed every layer: the veto only knows names somebody remembered to add, the allowlist saw a public tag, and the identifier scan found nothing. That is exactly the shape of that private-corpus ingest page, which only got caught in 13d because I hand-added `a private project` to the denylist. Deriving the denylist from the project registry does **not** fix it — the registry holds only 5 projects and contains none of the private ones this veto needs to know about. **Fix:** publishability now depends on ONE thing — a `project:` frontmatter field naming a project in `PUBLIC_PROJECTS`. Tags no longer confer it. An unlisted project is blocked **by construction**, so there is no list of private names to keep current; `PRIVATE_PROJECT_TAGS` survives only as non-load-bearing defence in depth and a clearer message. `memory.add_decision` now writes `project:` from the active workspace (falling back to `unknown`, which is blocked) so new decisions self-declare. Migrated the 10 tracked pages to declare their project.
- **Hole 2 — gitignore globs were case-sensitive while the CLI writes lowercase.** `wiki/claims/Claim-*.md` only ever matched the CLI's `claim-<id>.md` because macOS is case-insensitive; on Linux or in CI every CLI-written claim — each quoting a private source **verbatim** — was trackable. **Fix:** `wiki/claims/*` wholesale (same reasoning as `wiki/sources/`), `wiki/concepts/[Aa]uto[Cc]oncept-*.md`, `wiki/[Nn]ote-*.md`, and `wiki/claims/` added to `BLOCKED_PATTERNS` so the guard enforces what gitignore cannot for already-tracked files. Untracked the 17 generated fixture pages that gitignore could no longer protect (10 claims, 7 AutoConcept); the 5 hand-written concept pages stay tracked.
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/public_repo_guard.py` (+`page_project`, `PUBLIC_PROJECTS`, claims blocked, rewritten `page_verdict` and failure message), `scripts/memory.py` (+`current_project_name`, `project:` in decisions), `.gitignore` (case-folded globs), `tests/test_repo_guard_redteam.py` (rewritten for the declared-project design), 10 tracked pages migrated, 17 generated pages untracked, AGENTS.md + CHANGELOG.md + docs/agents-history.md.
- **Verification:** `pytest` -> **566 passed, 0 failed**. Guard passes; it FAILED first on all 10 tracked pages (no `project:`) and on the 10 tracked claims, both correctly. Case-robustness verified directly: `claim-abc.md`, `Claim-X.md`, `autoconcept-x.md`, `Note-a.md` all now ignored, while `wiki/claims/.gitkeep` and the hand-written `wiki/concepts/corrigibility.md` stay trackable. Against the real store the guard now blocks **85/85** untracked pages with **0** private leaking through. `git diff --check` clean. **Frozen provenance golds unchanged** (86%/100%/0%/100%/100%). Committed; **not pushed**.
- **Follow-ups:** (1) **The fail-closed default now blocks the ~57 publishable pages too**, because every untracked page predates the `project:` field. That is the correct direction — safe by default, friction on publish — but anyone wanting to publish one must add the field. (2) The guard still cannot read meaning: a private decision declared `project: zurvan` with no UUID in it would pass. The declaration is an author's assertion, and the guard checks only that the assertion names a public project. (3) `add_decision` derives `project:` from the ACTIVE workspace, which is not always the decision's subject — a Zurvan decision written while `a private project` is active gets `project: a private project` and is blocked (safe direction, but needs hand-correction).

### 2026-09-13d (Australia/Sydney) — Publication guard for cross-project pages, red-teamed
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Root-cause fix for the exposure found while reviewing the 83 untracked decision pages. Zurvan writes EVERY project's memory into one store, `wiki/decisions/` is tracked by design, and the remote is public — so the default was that a tenancy decision naming a third party, a clinical study's methodology, or a student's coursework was one `git add` from publication. Nothing but human attention stood in the way. Test-and-guard change; no retrieval/ranking change, so no `eval_search` re-run.
- **Summary:** `public_repo_guard` now decides whether a tracked page under `wiki/decisions/` or `wiki/digests/` may be published, in three layers, each added because red-teaming showed the previous one insufficient: (1) a `PRIVATE_PROJECT_TAGS` **veto**, (2) a `PUBLIC_PROJECT_TAGS` **allowlist** — allowlist not denylist, because a denylist fails OPEN for every new private project, which is precisely the case that matters — and (3) a scan for account UUIDs and support-ticket references. Fails closed throughout: an unreadable, empty, frontmatter-less or untagged page is reported, because the guard cannot show it is safe. Email addresses are reported as a NON-fatal note, since the guard cannot distinguish an author's address quoted from a published paper (the agentic-misalignment digest) from a private correspondent's.
- **Red team (the part that found the real defects).** 40 adversarial tests in `tests/test_repo_guard_redteam.py` attacking the check rather than confirming it — tags hidden in the body, a second frontmatter block, unterminated frontmatter, a Cyrillic homoglyph, substring lookalikes, nested/capitalised/non-`.md` paths, empty and malformed tag lists, and an unreadable page. **Three genuine defects surfaced and were fixed:** (a) the allowlist alone let **2 of 28** private pages through — one page is correctly tagged for two public projects yet carries an Anthropic **Organization ID** and a support-ticket ref, and another is correctly tagged `zurvan` yet concerns a private research corpus; that is what motivated layers (1) and (3). (b) The tag parser missed the bare `tags: a, b, c` form used by `wiki/digests/claudes-constitution.md`, reporting a **false positive on a correctly-tagged page** — a guard that cries wolf gets switched off. (c) Padding inside quotes survived parsing, so a padded private tag could ride beside a genuine public one; the veto test had been **passing for the wrong reason** (the page was blocked for having no public tag, so the veto never fired) and was rewritten to assert the private tag alongside a public one.
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/public_repo_guard.py` (+`page_tags`, `page_verdict`, `page_identifiers`, `audit_cross_project_pages`, `advisory_emails`, two tag sets), `tests/test_repo_guard_redteam.py` (new, 40 tests), `tests/test_check_gate_repo_guard.py` (one test rewritten — it asserted `main()` passes for `wiki/decisions/`, which the new layer correctly changes; it now tests the pattern layer it was actually about), and a `zurvan` tag added to the four legacy Zurvan decisions the guard flagged (`delay-vector-search`, `e2e-smoke-decision`, `ingest-large-pdfs…`, `mcp-write-mode-stays-disabled-by-default`) — correct metadata they were simply missing, not a workaround. AGENTS.md + CHANGELOG.md + docs/agents-history.md.
- **Verification:** `pytest` -> **565 passed, 0 failed**. Effectiveness measured against the real store rather than asserted: of the 85 untracked pages the guard blocks **28** and passes 57, and **0** of the pages identified as private in the review now slip through (it was 2 before layers 1 and 3). `public_repo_guard.py` passes on the working tree; it FAILED first, correctly flagging the four untagged legacy decisions and — via the parser bug — the constitution digest. `git diff --check` clean. **Frozen provenance golds unchanged**: 86% / 100% / 0% / 100% / 100%. Committed; **not pushed**.
- **Follow-ups:** (1) **The guard is a backstop, not a classifier.** It reasons about tags and identifier shapes; it cannot read meaning. A private decision correctly tagged for a public project with no UUID in it would still pass — the residual risk is real and the 59 pages judged publishable rest partly on my reading, not only on the guard. (2) `PRIVATE_PROJECT_TAGS` is hand-maintained and fails open for a project nobody has listed; the allowlist is the load-bearing half. (3) The same exposure applies to `wiki/note-*.md` and `wiki/claims/claim-*.md`: both are gitignored, but `wiki/claims/Claim-*.md` is a **case-sensitive** pattern that only matches the CLI's lowercase output on a case-insensitive filesystem — on Linux/CI every CLI-written claim becomes trackable. Legacy tracked examples of both already exist. Not fixed here. (4) The 83 private decision pages remain untracked and unpushed.

### 2026-09-13c (Australia/Sydney) — Close the wiki/sources leak path + fix four flagged defects
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** The follow-ups flagged across today's two earlier entries, fixed together. The first is a real privacy gap found while preparing a push to the public remote; the rest are correctness and hygiene defects. No retrieval/ranking change, so no `eval_search` re-run required.
- **Defect 1 (privacy) — a private source page was committable on a public remote.** `.gitignore` had `wiki/sources/*.md`, and `public_repo_guard`'s extension patterns covered only `.pdf`/`.docx`. Neither matches a sibling artefact, so `wiki/sources/anthropic-risk-report-august-2026.md.pre-repair.bak` — 534 KB carrying the full text of an ingested private document — was ignored by neither and blocked by neither. **Fix:** `.gitignore` now ignores `wiki/sources/*` with `!wiki/sources/.gitkeep`, and `wiki/sources/` is a directory entry in `BLOCKED_PATTERNS` so the whole subtree is blocked regardless of extension. Applying the new guard surfaced **7 already-tracked** fixture source pages (`e2e_smoke_note.txt.md`, `example.pdf.md`, `long_paper.pdf.md`, `medium_article.md.md`, `scanned_or_ugly.pdf.md`, `short_paper.pdf.md`, `small_note.txt.md`) — 359-704 byte stubs from 2026-05-30 derived from the repo's own `raw/` test fixtures. They are regenerated by the gauntlet and e2e scripts, are referenced by no code (only their `raw/` inputs are) and appear in no eval gold, so they were `git rm --cached`'d rather than exempted: exempting seven paths would have weakened the invariant permanently. They remain on disk and are now ignored.
- **Defect 2 (correctness) — ingest deduped by path, not content hash.** `sources.path` is the UNIQUE column, so the same document copied to a second filename ingested twice, contradicting the documented hash dedup. **Fix:** new `db.find_source_by_hash()`; `register_source` now refuses on a duplicate content hash, and `ingest.main` reports the path the content was already ingested under instead of a bare "already been ingested".
- **Defect 3 (test rot) — `test_find_stale_decisions` was a calendar bomb.** Its fixture pinned `created_at` to `2026-05-01`, which drifted past the 90-day staleness window it asserts on, so `find_stale_decisions` correctly returned 2 where the test expected 1. This failed in both earlier entries today and was each time proven pre-existing by stashing. **Fix:** the date is now `datetime.now() - timedelta(days=10)`, inside the window regardless of run date. The suite is green for the first time today.
- **Defect 4 (hygiene) — `question add` wrote a trailing space.** An empty tag list rendered `- **Tags**: `, which `git diff --check` reported on every commit touching `wiki/open-questions.md`. **Fix:** the tag line is `rstrip()`ed; the two pre-existing offending lines were cleaned. Also renumbered `check.sh`'s gate banners, which ran `[1/7] [2/7] [2/6] ... [14/15] [15/16]` and are now a consistent `[n/23]`, and added `.coverage`, `.coverage.*`, `htmlcov/`, `coverage.json` to `.gitignore`.
- **Files Changed:** `.gitignore`, `scripts/public_repo_guard.py`, `scripts/db.py`, `scripts/ingest.py`, `scripts/memory.py`, `scripts/check.sh`, `tests/test_check_gate_repo_guard.py` (+8 tests), `tests/test_ingest.py` (+4), `tests/test_memory.py` (+2), `tests/test_decision_compare.py` (fixture), plus 7 `wiki/sources/*.md` untracked. The two pre-existing trailing-whitespace lines in `wiki/open-questions.md` were stripped **locally only** — that file's uncommitted diff adds 91 lines of private cross-project content (venue queries for an event, university coursework including a tutor's remarks, an unpublished clinical study), so it was deliberately left unstaged on a public remote. AGENTS.md + CHANGELOG.md + docs/agents-history.md (this entry + rotation).
- **Verification:** `pytest` -> **525 passed, 0 failed** — the first fully green suite today; the calendar bomb that failed in both earlier entries is gone. `public_repo_guard.py` passes (it failed first, correctly catching the 7 legacy tracked pages). `git diff --check` clean across the **whole tree** for the first time. Leak fix verified against the real artefact: `git check-ignore -q` now reports the `.bak` ignored, `BLOCKED_PATTERNS` matches it via `wiki/sources/`, `wiki/sources/.gitkeep` stays trackable (the negation resolves), and nothing under `wiki/sources` appears as untracked. **Frozen provenance golds re-verified unchanged**: 86% recall / 100% completeness / 0% raw leak / 100% hash / 100% graph.
- **Follow-ups:** (1) The 7 untracked fixture pages disappear from the public repo on this push — intended, but worth knowing if anyone cloned expecting them. (2) `check.sh` still mutates the live repo (gates 11, 14-22 write real sessions, snapshots, packs and reports; gate 21 binds a port); the pytest equivalents added earlier today are isolated and it should delegate to them. (3) `scripts/e2e_mcp_smoke.py` remains 0% covered and needs a `run_smoke(client)` extraction to be testable. (4) The 83 untracked private `wiki/decisions/*.md` are still unreviewed and deliberately unpushed.

### 2026-09-13b (Australia/Sydney) — Comprehensive test coverage derived from scripts/check.sh (72.1% -> 83.3%)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Test-only change (no production code touched). `scripts/check.sh` has 22 gates but only gate [2/7] runs pytest; the other 21 are shell smoke steps that (a) execute against the **live** repo — mutating `wiki/`, writing snapshots, binding port 8769 — and (b) run as subprocesses, so none of that code is measured. That is why `scripts/cli.py` read as 51% covered despite being the most-exercised module in the project. These tests turn each gate's contract into isolated pytest coverage.
- **Summary:** Six new `tests/test_check_gate_*.py` files, **226 new tests** (284 -> 510 passing). The CLI file drives `cli.main()` **in-process** with a patched `sys.argv` and stubbed collaborators, which is both what makes the code measurable and what keeps the tests from mutating the wiki — a deliberate split of responsibility, since the CLI's own job is argument parsing, dispatch and exit codes, and the collaborators already have their own test files. Negative paths are covered throughout: the repo guard must *fail* on every blocked pattern and *pass* on `docs/*.pdf` and `raw/.gitkeep`; `rebuild_index` must exclude gitignored pages (the leak that previously put private document filenames into a tracked `index.md`); `project current`, `report validate`, `publish validate`, `evidence inspect` and the shelled `eval`/`audit` commands must all exit non-zero rather than pass silently; `trace_node` must terminate on a cycle; an already-redacted evidence pack must never be re-redacted.
- **Files Changed:** new `tests/test_check_gate_repo_guard.py` (23 tests), `tests/test_check_gate_audit_wiki.py` (17), `tests/test_check_gate_index_rebuild.py` (10), `tests/test_check_gate_schemas.py` (25), `tests/test_check_gate_cli_smoke.py` (90), `tests/test_check_gate_policy_radar.py` (20), `tests/test_check_gate_graph_query.py` (13), `tests/test_check_gate_evidence_export.py` (25). AGENTS.md + CHANGELOG.md + docs/agents-history.md (this entry + rotation of the 2026-07-22 entry).
- **Verification:** `pytest` -> **510 passed, 1 failed**; the failure is the same pre-existing calendar bomb `test_decision_compare.py::test_find_stale_decisions` documented in the 2026-09-13 entry (fixture dates vs a 90-day window measured from today), unrelated to these files. Coverage measured before and after with pytest-cov 6.0.0 over `--cov=scripts`: **72.1% -> 83.3%** (1532 -> 915 missing of 5488 statements; 617 newly covered). Excluding `scripts/e2e_mcp_smoke.py`, which is a 295-line subprocess-only script with a single function, the figure is **85.5%**. Per module: public_repo_guard 0->97%, schemas 0->100%, rebuild_index 0->97%, audit_wiki 28->99%, policy_radar 37->95%, evidence_export 49->100%, graph_query 58->100%, cli 51->96%. 12 of 78 modules now at 100%, 42 at >=90%. `public_repo_guard.py` passed; `git diff --check` clean on `tests/`. No production code changed, so the frozen provenance golds are untouched. Committed and pushed to the public remote on Raouf's explicit request (`ZURVAN_ALLOW_PUSH=1`), after confirming the pushed tree carries no `wiki/sources`, `raw/`, `.bak`, coverage or `.obsidian` content and no secrets or personal data.
- **Follow-ups:** (1) **check.sh itself still mutates the live repo** — gates [10], [13], [14]-[21] write real sessions, snapshots, packs and reports into the working tree and `~/.zurvan`, and gate [20] binds a port. The pytest equivalents are isolated; check.sh should eventually delegate to them rather than re-running the mutating shell steps. (2) `scripts/e2e_mcp_smoke.py` (139 statements, 0%) is not unit-testable as written: it is module-level script code around one helper. Smallest safe refactor would be extracting a `run_smoke(client)` function so the assertions can run against an in-process MCP session; deliberately not done here, since refactoring production code was outside a test-only change. (3) Next-largest gaps after this pass: `mcp_tools` 46%, `evidence_collect` 53%, `extract` 64%, `eval_search` 71%, `review_routes` 73%. (4) check.sh's gate numbering is inconsistent ([1/7], [2/6], [7/10], [14/15] ...) — cosmetic, but it makes the script hard to read as a checklist.

### 2026-09-13 (Australia/Sydney) — Fix ingest half-state on crash + audit's over-broad "uncited claim" heuristic
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Two code defects surfaced while ingesting Anthropic's *An alignment assessment of recent cybersecurity incidents* (2026-09-09). Both were real bugs, not misuse. No retrieval/ranking change, so no `eval_search` re-run required.
- **Defect 1 — a failed ingest poisoned its own source path.** `main()` called `register_source()` *before* any work, and dedup is keyed on **path**, not content hash (`db.py:20` — `path TEXT UNIQUE`). `append_log` reaches PyYAML lazily through `wiki_merge` → `safe_write` at the very END of the run, so on an interpreter without PyYAML the ingest wrote the source page, registered the path, then died on the import. Every retry was then refused with "has already been ingested" while no log entry existed. **Fix (two parts):** (a) new `require_ingest_dependencies()` preflight in `scripts/ingest.py` probes `import yaml` before anything is written and exits 1 with an actionable message naming `scripts/zurvan` and `ZURVAN_PYTHON` — mirroring the interpreter probe added to the wrapper on 2026-07-22; (b) the page/stub/log/index writes are wrapped so any `BaseException` calls the new `scripts/db.unregister_source()` and re-raises, leaving the path free for a retry. The up-front `register_source` claim is kept as the double-ingest guard, so the happy path is unchanged.
- **Defect 2 — `zurvan audit` flagged every CLI-written claim as uncited.** `audit_uncited_claims` accepted a citation only as the literal `"cited from"`/`"missing?"` phrasing or a Markdown inline link `[..](..)`. But `zurvan claim add` cites via `source:` frontmatter plus an Obsidian `**Source**: [[wikilink]]` and emits no Markdown link — so 235 of 956 claim pages were reported as uncited despite every one carrying a validated, verbatim-checked source. **Fix:** extracted `claim_is_cited()`, which now also accepts non-empty `source:` frontmatter and wikilinks. One subtlety caught by its own regression test: the frontmatter regex must use `[ \t]*`, not `\s*` — `\s` spans the newline, so a bare `source:` matched whatever the following line started with and an empty source counted as cited.
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/ingest.py` (preflight + rollback), `scripts/db.py` (+`unregister_source`), `scripts/audit_wiki.py` (+`claim_is_cited`, rewritten `audit_uncited_claims`), `tests/test_ingest.py` (+2 regression tests), `tests/test_audit.py` (+2 regression tests), AGENTS.md + CHANGELOG.md + docs/agents-history.md (this entry + rotation of the 2026-07-08 entry).
- **Verification:** `pytest` → **284 passed, 1 failed**. The sole failure, `test_decision_compare.py::test_find_stale_decisions`, is a **pre-existing calendar bomb, proven not caused by this change**: `git stash`ing all five edited files reproduces it identically. Its fixture hardcodes decision dates (2026-05-01, 2026-06-05) against a `days=90` window measured from today; as of 2026-09-13 the 2026-06-05 decision has crossed 90 days, so `find_stale_decisions` correctly returns 2 where the test asserts 1. `decision_compare` imports none of the modules touched here. Defect 1 verified live: `PYTHONPATH=. python3 -m scripts.ingest <pdf>` (Homebrew 3.14, no PyYAML) now prints the guidance and exits **1** without writing or registering anything. Defect 2 verified live: uncited-claim warnings **235 → 0**, total audit issues 2113 → 1879, and an independent scan confirms all 956 claim pages genuinely carry a citation (0 with none of frontmatter/wikilink/md-link/legacy phrasing), so 0 is correct rather than the check being disabled. `public_repo_guard.py` passed; `git diff --check` clean on all five files (the two remaining hits are pre-existing untagged entries in `wiki/open-questions.md`, not mine — my two questions carry tags). **Frozen provenance golds re-verified unchanged**: 86% recall / 100% completeness / 0% raw leak / 100% hash / 100% graph. Committed and pushed to the public remote on Raouf's explicit request (`ZURVAN_ALLOW_PUSH=1`); the 83 untracked private decision pages, the `wiki/sources/*.bak` and coverage artifacts were deliberately left out of the push.
- **Follow-ups:** (1) `test_find_stale_decisions` should pin `datetime.now` or compute its fixture dates relative to today — it will keep drifting. (2) `question add` writes `- **Tags**: ` with a trailing space when no tags are given, which is what `git diff --check` keeps reporting in `wiki/open-questions.md`; out of scope here. (3) Source dedup is by path, not content hash, despite the docs describing hash dedup — the same file at two paths ingests twice. (4) Still open from the ingest itself: `scripts/ingest.py` captures no content from image-only PDF figures (filed as an open question).

### 2026-07-22 (Australia/Sydney) — Fix `zurvan` wrapper interpreter (PyYAML ModuleNotFoundError) + line-by-line ingest of "Agentic Misalignment in Summer 2026"
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Two things. (1) **CODE FIX:** `scripts/zurvan` hardcoded `exec python3`, which on this machine resolves to Homebrew Python 3.14 — a PEP 668 externally-managed interpreter with no PyYAML — so every `zurvan …` invocation (and the existing `test_zurvan_wrapper_works_from_any_cwd` test) died with `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'yaml'`. (2) **CONTENT INGEST** (no further code change): a full 39-page read of the Alignment Science Blog post *Agentic Misalignment in Summer 2026* (Lynch, Hughes, Serrano, Kirk, Bowman; 2026-07-13; Anthropic Fellows program). **No LLM extraction** (mock off) — read + authored, so every claim quote is verbatim-present in the source page.
- **Summary (fix):** Wrapper now selects an interpreter that can actually `import yaml`: honors an explicit `ZURVAN_PYTHON` override, else picks the first of `python3`/`python` that imports yaml, else falls back to `python3` (historical default preserved). Backward-compatible — on a box where `python3` has the deps, behavior is unchanged. Added regression test `test_zurvan_wrapper_honors_python_override`. Root cause: `python3`→`/opt/homebrew/bin/python3` (3.14, no deps); working interpreter is `/opt/anaconda3/bin/python` (3.12, has PyYAML + sentence_transformers) — the same one the SessionStart hook already uses. Did NOT `pip install --break-system-packages` into 3.14 (rejected as fragile).
- **Summary (ingest):** Real pipeline: `raw/papers/agentic-misalignment-summer-2026.pdf` → `python -m scripts.ingest` → `wiki/sources/agentic-misalignment-summer-2026.md` (92 KB) → `index search` + `graph rebuild`. Authored tracked self-contained digest `wiki/digests/agentic-misalignment-summer-2026.md` covering tl;dr + the four failure modes (covert sabotage / assisting fraud / motivated mislabeling / coaching human proxies to whistleblow), every case study, all figure numbers, appendices A–F, notes 1–11, and Simurgh relevance. Filed **1 tracked decision** + **5 evidence-backed atomic claims** (each `evidence` verified verbatim-present: Gemini 3.1 Pro 19/20 & 11 covert; mislabeling definition; Opus 4.7/4.6 83.3%/62.2%; record-tampering common in non-Claude models; "Atlas equips an employee"). Numbers persisted incl. Opus 4.8 mislabel 74.4%→3.3% on consequence reversal; Fig 2 record-tampering DeepSeek V4 20/20 … Sonnet 4.6 0/20; Fig 7 external disclosure Opus 4.7 3/20.
- **Files Changed (tracked):** `scripts/zurvan` (interpreter select), `tests/test_cli.py` (+1 regression test), `wiki/digests/agentic-misalignment-summer-2026.md` (new), `wiki/decisions/ingested-anthropic-agentic-misalignment-in-summer-.md` (new), AGENTS.md + CHANGELOG.md + docs/agents-history.md (this entry + rotation of Phase 27-step-2). Local/gitignored: `raw/papers/…pdf`, `wiki/sources/…md`, 5 `wiki/claims/claim-*.md`, rebuilt `data/{search,graph}.sqlite`.
- **Verification:** Wrapper fix confirmed live — `zurvan version` now works (auto-picks `python`; `python3` still lacks yaml) and honors `ZURVAN_PYTHON`. `pytest` → **280 passed, 1 failed**. The sole failure `test_hybrid_search.py::test_rebuild_reuses_embeddings_for_unchanged_chunks` is a **pre-existing test-isolation fragility, provably not caused by this change** (my edits are a bash wrapper + a CLI subprocess test; the test imports only `scripts.rebuild_search_index` and never runs my code). Diagnosed via isolated re-run: embedding **reuse works** (the second rebuild reused 23912 embeddings), but the corpus grows **+5 chunks between the test's two back-to-back full-corpus rebuilds (24185→24190)** because rebuild/CLI activity appends to indexed wiki files (`wiki/log.md`, `wiki/index.md` — the documented "tests write to wiki/log.md" isolation leak), so 5 changed chunks get re-embedded and the strict `calls == 1` assert fails (`assert 6 == 1`). Warm `index search` on the real corpus reuses 23843/24185. **Frozen provenance golds re-verified unchanged** (86% recall / 100% completeness / 0% raw leak / 100% hash / 100% graph). `public_repo_guard.py` clean; `git diff --check` clean on my files (the only whitespace hits are in the user's pre-existing uncommitted `wiki/open-questions.md`). Hybrid search returns the new digest/source on top (0.92). Not committed/pushed.
- **Follow-ups:** (1) The embedding-reuse=0 breakage predates this work and deserves its own investigation — filed as an open question. (2) The repo carried a large PRE-EXISTING uncommitted backlog before this session (Phase 28 changelog entry + ~15 untracked `wiki/decisions/*.md` ingests + dirty `wiki/open-questions.md`) — left intact, not reconciled here. (3) 5 atomic `claim-*.md` are gitignored → local-only; the digest is self-contained.

### 2026-07-08 (Australia/Sydney) — Line-by-line ingest of Anthropic's "Verbalizable Representations Form a Global Workspace in Language Models"
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Phase 28 — content ingestion (no code change): a full line-by-line read of Anthropic's Transformer Circuits paper (Gurnee, Sofroniew, … Lindsey; 2026-07-06; 145 pp) into structured, evidence-backed memory. **No LLM extraction** (mock off) — I read the source and authored the knowledge, so every claim quote is verbatim-present in the source page.
- **Summary:** Real pipeline: `raw/papers/…pdf` → `python -m scripts.ingest` → `wiki/sources/…md` (660 KB) + deterministic AutoConcept/Claim scaffold stubs → `index search` (+745) + `graph rebuild`. Authored a tracked, self-contained digest `wiki/digests/verbalizable-representations-global-workspace.md` (thesis → J-lens method → 5 functional properties → structure → alignment auditing → Assistant's perspective → Counterfactual Reflection Training → limitations → Simurgh relevance), per the constitution-digest convention (claims + source pages are gitignored, so the tracked knowledge is self-contained). Filed **8 evidence-backed atomic claims** (each `evidence` verified verbatim-present; one removed + re-added when its quote didn't support the text), **1 tracked decision** (paper = interpretability-side twin of Simurgh's verification-side thesis), **2 open questions**. Quotes recovered from a per-character letter-spacing PDF artifact preserved verbatim in the source; digest de-artifacts. Persisted numbers: J-lens = mean Jacobian × unembedding over ~1000 prompts; J-space component = median 6–7% variance yet carries reportability; workspace band ~L38–L92; eval-awareness ablation 71%→3% + blackmail 0/180→13/180; reflection training 0.25→0.07 & 0.38→0.05, ablation reverses; workspace predates the "self."
- **Files Changed:** `wiki/digests/verbalizable-representations-global-workspace.md` (new, tracked), `wiki/decisions/anthropics-j-lens--global-workspace-paper-is-the-i.md` (new, tracked), `wiki/open-questions.md` (+2). ### 2026-07-07 (Australia/Sydney) — Zurvan as the global brain: implementation (Phase 27, step 2)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Phase 27, step 2 — implemented the approved global-brain design end-to-end (code + user-level hook/MCP/CLAUDE.md rollout). Executed `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-06-zurvan-global-brain.md` inline via the executing-plans skill.
- **Summary:** (1) `project_digest(project, root=None)` in `scripts/agent_workflow.py` — a lean, ~150-token keyword-scan recall digest over `wiki/decisions|claims|open-questions`; **never loads the embedding model** (matching: case-insensitive, `_`→`-`, substring-or-hyphen-token≥4-equals-tag; caps 5 decisions/3 claims/3 questions, 120-char lines); ~0.1 s live. (2) `agent_prime(project=None, fix_stale=False)` — `fix_stale` runs the existing incremental `rebuild_search_index()` only when `_index_staleness()` says STALE, degrading to the warning on failure; `project` routes to the digest. (3) CLI `zurvan agent prime [--for-project NAME] [--fix-stale]` — **flag named `--for-project`, not `--project`**, because `cli.py` reserves a global `--project <workspace>` switch it preprocesses before argparse (plan said `--project`; renamed to avoid the collision and threaded through hook + tests). (4) Rollout (user-level, outside git): repo `.claude/settings.json` hook gains `--fix-stale`; global `~/.claude/settings.json` gets a `startup|clear` SessionStart hook that skips the Zurvan repo, calls the self-locating `scripts/zurvan` wrapper, `2>/dev/null || true`, `timeout 120`; fixed the user-scope `~/.claude.json` `mcpServers.zurvan` env bug (`ZURVAN_MCP_READONLY 0→1`, `ZURVAN_EMBED_PROVIDER mock→sentence_transformers`); created `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` recall/write-back section; symlinked `zurvan` onto PATH. **No retrieval-ranking or index-format change** → per the standing invariant, no `eval_search` re-run required. **MCP-diet advisory (report only, nothing removed):** 9 user-scope servers (Scholar-Gateway, crawl4ai, mempalace, reasoning-engine, ssh-mcp, ssh1-3, zurvan) — `mempalace` overlaps Zurvan's memory role, and 4 near-identical SSH servers could collapse to those actually used (best practice ≈ 5-6 active; each costs a subprocess + schema tokens per session).
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/agent_workflow.py`, `scripts/cli.py`, `.claude/settings.json`, `tests/test_agent_workflow.py` (+7), `tests/test_cli.py` (+2). User-level (untracked): `~/.claude/settings.json`, `~/.claude.json`, `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` (+ `.bak-2026-07-06` backups), PATH symlink.
- **Verification:** `pytest` → **280 passed, 0 failed** (+9). E2E: global hook from a non-Zurvan repo emits the digest (exit 0, ~0.1 s when fresh; reindexes when stale); guard suppresses output inside the Zurvan repo; `claude mcp list` shows `zurvan` ✔ connected from another dir; read-only asserted (`ZURVAN_MCP_READONLY == "1"`). **Frozen provenance golds re-verified unchanged** (86% recall, 100% completeness, 0% raw leak, 100% graph presence). `public_repo_guard.py` + `git diff --check` clean. No `eval_search` re-run (no ranking/indexing change).
- **Follow-ups:** MCP diet is advisory — Raouf to decide whether to drop `mempalace`/collapse SSH servers. `rebuild_search_index()` prints progress to stdout, so a stale-index session start injects two extra lines into context (harmless, only when stale). Consider a global write-back reminder if manual write-back proves too easy to skip from other repos.

Filed **15 evidence-backed atomic claims** via `add_claim` (each `evidence` verified verbatim-present in the source page) and **5 tracked concept pages** (`the-principal-hierarchy`, `corrigibility`, `hard-constraints`, `honesty-standards`, `the-1000-users-heuristic`), plus **3 open questions** the document itself raises. Rebuilt search index (+119 embeddings) and graph (879 nodes / 1102 edges). Note: `claim-*.md` files are gitignored on macOS via the case-insensitive `Claim-*` rule, so the 15 atomic claims are local-only searchable memory (like `wiki/sources/`); the digest embeds every key quote inline, so the tracked knowledge is self-contained.

Local/gitignored: 15 `wiki/claims/claim-*.md`, rebuilt `data/search.sqlite` + `data/graph.sqlite`.
- **Verification:** Hybrid search for "hard constraints and corrigibility" returns the constitution source (0.93) and digest (0.87) top; all 5 concepts + digest indexed; `public_repo_guard.py` + `git diff --check` clean. Frozen provenance golds untouched (content-only change).

### 2026-07-04 (Australia/Sydney) — Remaining-functions audit (write path) + `zurvan` operating skill
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Phase 25 — audit the remaining core modules (memory/write path + infra: `memory`, `safe_write`, `wiki_merge`, `db`, `filename_utils`, `validate_extraction`, `config`, `workspace`, `llm`); smoke the research/publication subsystem entry points; ship a project skill so any LLM works with Zurvan professionally
- **Summary:** All 78 scripts compile. `llm.py` (provider layer), `db.py`, `config.py`, `workspace.py`, `filename_utils.py`, `validate_extraction.py`, `wiki_merge.py` are sound; research/publication CLI entry points (`version`, `doctor`, `trace/evidence/report/project list`, `graph stats`, `federation stats`) all run without runtime errors. **Two real write-path defects fixed.** **(W1 — data corruption, `safe_write.py`)** `escape_yaml_string` used `yaml.dump` with default width, which **line-wraps long scalars**; the naive line-by-line frontmatter parsers (`graph_build.parse_frontmatter`, `wiki_merge._parse_fm`) then truncate a wrapped `title:`/`status:` value at the first line. Added `width=2**20, allow_unicode=True` so values stay on one line (round-trips through a real YAML parser). **(W2 — silent data loss, `memory.py`)** `add_decision`/`add_note` derived the filename purely from the title slug, so two titles slugging identically (`Use SQLite!` vs `Use SQLite?` → `use-sqlite`) **overwrote each other** (`write_file_safely` uses mode `w`). Added `_unique_path` (numeric suffix on collision), matching how claims/questions already avoid collisions. Non-defects noted: `merge_extraction`/`extract`/`audit_wiki` still write via CWD-relative `wiki/` (safe through the `zurvan` CLI which anchors `cwd=PROJECT_ROOT`; known follow-up); `is_valid_zurvan_project` requires `scripts/`, so knowledge-only federated projects work via `search-all` but not `--project X` (by design); `safe_write.is_safe_path` raw-block is case-sensitive like the read path was (write targets are fixed `wiki/` locations, low risk). **Skill:** added `.claude/skills/zurvan/SKILL.md` — a professional operating manual (first-30-seconds orientation, the 6 hard rules, mental model, read/write/ingest paths, the mandatory change protocol, environment, and the gotchas learned across Phases 21–25: ambient-embed-env test dependency, `wiki/log.md` test-noise reverts, leave `.obsidian/graph.json`, guard blocks `.pdf`, gitignored ingest pages, frozen-gold re-verify).
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/safe_write.py`, `scripts/memory.py`, `tests/test_memory.py` (+2), `tests/test_cli.py` (loosened over-specific note-filename assertion for collision-safety), `.claude/skills/zurvan/SKILL.md` (new).
- **Verification:** `pytest` → **271 passed, 0 failed** (+2). `escape_yaml_string` on a long value now has no newline and round-trips; two colliding decision titles now produce two files (`…-2.md`). Frozen provenance golds unchanged (86% recall, 100% completeness, 0% raw leak). `public_repo_guard.py` + `git diff --check` clean. Research subsystem entry points smoke-run clean.
- **Follow-ups:** Deep line-by-line of the full research/publication subsystem (`review_*`, `report_*`, `publish/publication`, `evidence_*`, `decision_*`, `contradiction_radar`, `policy_*`, `snapshot`, `federation`, `install_mcp_config`, `e2e_mcp_smoke`) is still worthwhile — this pass smoke-tested them, not line-audited. Migrate `ingest`/`extract`/`audit_wiki` off CWD-relative `wiki/` writes. Consider a periodic test-isolation cleanup so `wiki/log.md`/`note-*.md` stop accumulating.

### 2026-07-04 (Australia/Sydney) — Graph + trace audit (3 render/consistency fixes)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Phase 24 — file-by-file audit of the graph stack (`graph_schema`, `graph_query`, `graph_context`, `graph_export`, `graph_build`) and the trace stack (`trace_schema`, `trace_writer`, `trace_validate`, `trace_replay`, `eval_provenance`, CLI trace handlers)
- **Summary:** Both stacks are largely sound. Trace core is well-built and left untouched (it is frozen-adjacent): `hash_payload` canonical JSON, `trace_validate` checks required fields + id regexes + duplicate event_ids + payload-hash integrity, `TraceStore` enforces safe ids and `_ensure_under` traversal guards, `eval_provenance` invariant gate (raw-leak / hash-integrity / validation) — all correct; CLI `trace list/inspect/validate/replay` handle errors with proper exit codes. Graph schema has the right indexes. Three real defects fixed. **(G1 — render, `trace_replay.py`)** `replay_trace_file` renders each event payload into a Markdown table cell without escaping `|`; a payload value containing a pipe broke the table (2 of 103 committed trace events currently affected). Pipes are now escaped `\|` (GFM un-escapes inside the cell); verified on real `trace-…-real0004`. **(G2 — render, `graph_export.py`)** `export_dot` wrote node/edge labels with unescaped `"`/`\`, so a title with a quote produced malformed DOT Graphviz can't parse (0 titles have `"` today but 36 contain a backslash → malformed labels). Added `_dot_escape` (backslash then quote); live DOT export of 1975 labels now has 0 unbalanced-quote lines. **(G3 — consistency, `graph_build.py`)** `build_graph` indexed 24 `wiki/traces/` replay-mirror pages as graph nodes, even though the search chunker excludes `traces/` as derived + self-referential; added `traces` to `exclude_dirs`. Graph rebuild 879→855 nodes (−24 mirrors), 1096 edges. Non-defects noted: `graph_query.trace_node` is a "placeholder" whose DFS includes edges one hop past `depth` and dedups O(n²) (low-traffic, left); `graph_context.type_rank` has dead branches for node types graph_build never emits (`contradiction`/`open_question`); `eval_provenance` resolves each trace path twice (micro-inefficiency).
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/trace_replay.py`, `scripts/graph_export.py`, `scripts/graph_build.py`, `tests/test_trace_replay.py` (+1), `tests/test_graph_export.py` (+1), `tests/test_graph_build.py` (+1).
- **Verification:** `pytest` → **269 passed, 0 failed** (+3). Live: real pipe-containing trace replays to a well-formed 5-column row; real DOT export well-formed; `graph rebuild` → 855 nodes / 1096 edges with 0 `wiki/traces/` nodes. **Frozen provenance golds unchanged** (2C 86% recall, 100% completeness, 0% raw leak, 100% graph presence) — they score committed trace JSON, not the live graph, so G3 has no effect on them. `public_repo_guard.py` + `git diff --check` clean.
- **Follow-ups:** `trace_node` could be promoted from placeholder to a real bounded traversal if `zurvan graph trace` gets used; `graph_context.type_rank` dead branches can be dropped or backed by real contradiction/question node types.

### 2026-07-04 (Australia/Sydney) — CLI + search audit (keyword-search docs blind spot)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Phase 23 — file-by-file audit of the CLI (`cli.py`) and the search stack (`hybrid_search`, `context_export._search_internal`, `embed`, `rebuild_search_index`, `eval_search`, legacy `query`/`rebuild_index`)
- **Summary:** The CLI is sound — `_run_script` propagates child exit codes (verified: `eval validate-gold` on a missing path → exit 1), the `search`/`context`/`eval` handlers pass args correctly, and every subcommand referenced by a handler is registered (incl. `eval validate-gold`, line 369). Search core is sound too: `search_hybrid` embeds the query with the provider/model **stored in the index**, `_index_embedding_config` makes the index the source of truth, incremental rebuild reuse works. **One real defect fixed (`context_export._search_internal`, keyword mode):** it globbed **`wiki/` only**, so `zurvan search <term>` (without `--hybrid`) silently could not find any `docs/` page — even though hybrid mode, the chunk index (`scan_markdown_files` → wiki + docs), and `eval_search`'s own keyword branch all include `docs/`. Confirmed live: a query for text living only in `docs/ENVIRONMENT.md`/`docs/API.md` returned only `wiki/` notes. It also returned **absolute** `source_path`s (leaking machine paths into stdout and saved syntheses) while hybrid returned repo-relative. Fixed both: keyword mode now scans `wiki/` + `docs/` and returns repo-relative paths (idempotent with federation's `_relative_source_path`, so cross-project search is unaffected). Non-defects noted for the record: the `hybrid_score > 0.2` cutoff is weak because `(cos+1)/2` lifts orthogonal chunks to 0.5 (quality nuance, not touched — it's a ranking change); `embed.get_embedding` silently returns mock for unsupported providers (openai/anthropic aren't embed providers); legacy `query.py` is dead (not CLI-wired); `index rebuild` (nav `index.md`) vs `index search` (retrieval index) are confusingly named but documented and CWD-safe via `_run_script(cwd=PROJECT_ROOT)`.
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/context_export.py` (`_search_internal` keyword branch), `tests/test_context_export.py` (+1 keyword-finds-docs regression; +2 pre-existing non-hermetic format-table tests pinned to a tmp corpus — their sentinel term literally lives in a `docs/` plan file, so scanning docs/ correctly surfaced it).
- **Verification:** `pytest` → **266 passed, 0 failed** (+1) under the ambient env. Live: keyword `zurvan search` now returns `docs/API.md` #1 for a docs-only query with all-relative paths; `eval_search --hybrid --min-top3 0.6` unchanged (top-1 67% / top-3 100% / MRR 0.778) — keyword `_search_internal` is not on the hybrid gate path, so no ranking delta. Frozen provenance golds unchanged. `public_repo_guard.py` + `git diff --check` clean.
- **Follow-ups:** Keyword scoring is still crude (unique-keyword presence, substring, no field weighting) — fine as the non-hybrid fallback; hybrid is the real retriever. Consider a `--min-score` or revisiting the `0.2` cutoff if hybrid ever returns weak long tails (would need a documented eval re-run).

### 2026-07-04 (Australia/Sydney) — MCP + Obsidian audit (3 fixes + a test-isolation fix)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Phase 22 — file-by-file audit of the MCP server and Obsidian-compatibility layer; fix confirmed defects
- **Summary:** MCP layer is otherwise sound — server boots, 12 tools / 6 resources + 1 template / 4 prompts register, traversal/absolute paths blocked, writes blocked by default, resources point to real files; `.obsidian/` config valid (`useMarkdownLinks:false` → native `[[wikilinks]]`, sensible ignore filters). Three real defects fixed, two of them confirmed live. **(F1 — security, HIGH, `mcp_security.py`)** `enforce_read_only` blocked only the exact string `"1"`, so `ZURVAN_MCP_READONLY=true`/`yes`/empty **enabled writes** (fail-open) — a user setting `=true` to lock it would unlock it. Now fails **closed**: writes allowed only when the value is exactly `"0"` (whitespace-trimmed); every other value stays read-only. **(F2 — security, MEDIUM/macOS, `mcp_security.py`)** the `raw/` block was case-sensitive, so `is_safe_path("Raw/secret.md")` returned True and an agent could read untrusted `raw/` content by changing case on a case-insensitive filesystem; the top-level component is now compared case-insensitively. **(F3 — Obsidian compat, `graph_build.py`)** the wikilink parser didn't strip Obsidian's `[[target|alias]]` / `[[target#heading]]` syntax, so aliased/heading links never resolved to a node and their graph edges were silently dropped; alias/heading are now stripped before matching (corpus has 0 such links today, so this is future-proofing the "Obsidian-compatible" claim). **(F4 — test isolation, `tests/test_hybrid_search.py`)** uncovered while running the gate: the repo's own `.claude/settings.json` exports `ZURVAN_EMBED_PROVIDER=sentence_transformers`, which leaked into pytest and made the module-scoped `tmp_index` fixture build a real (slow, non-deterministic) index — breaking `test_query_embedding_follows_index_provider` (which asserts the index stores `mock`) whenever the suite runs without a manual `unset`. The fixture now pins the mock provider for the build and restores the prior env; the suite is hermetically green under the ambient session env and faster (115s→72s).
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/mcp_security.py`, `scripts/graph_build.py`, `tests/test_mcp_security.py` (+2), `tests/test_graph_build.py` (+1), `tests/test_hybrid_search.py` (fixture).
- **Verification:** `pytest` → **265 passed, 0 failed** (+3) under the ambient `ZURVAN_EMBED_PROVIDER=sentence_transformers` env (previously 1 failed without a manual unset). Live re-checks: `ZURVAN_MCP_READONLY=true` now blocks writes; `is_safe_path("Raw/…")` now False; MCP server still boots with 12 tools; `graph rebuild` OK (879 nodes / 1096 edges). Frozen provenance golds unchanged (2C 86% recall, 100% completeness, 0% raw leak). `public_repo_guard.py` + `git diff --check` clean.
- **Follow-ups:** No ranking/index change here, so no `eval_search` delta required. `zurvan_read_page`/`resource_file` cap files at 256 KB — a fully-ingested large PDF source page (e.g. the 194 KB constitution) is under the cap but a bigger one would be rejected; consider paging if that becomes real. Obsidian `templates.json` points at `wiki/templates` (exists); leave as-is.

### 2026-07-04 (Australia/Sydney) — Ingest "Claude's Constitution" + full-ingestion fixes
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Phase 21 — ingest the Claude's Constitution PDF fully into local memory; fix two defects that made "full" ingestion impossible (truncated source text + unsplittable large chunks)
- **Summary:** Ingested `raw/papers/claudes-constitution_webPDF_26-02.02a.pdf` (191,856 chars, clean pypdf extraction) into local memory: registered source, full-text source page, stub concept/claim, **203 retrieval chunks**. **No LLM extraction run** — provider is `mock` (no API keys, no ollama), and mock extraction would fabricate claims/concepts citing the constitution, violating the no-fabrication rule. **(Fix 1 — `ingest.py`)** `create_source_page` stored only `text[:1000]` (a preview) → now stores the **complete** extracted text so the whole document is chunked and searchable. Added `source_page_stem()`: derived pages use a sanitised, extension-free slug so PDF/DOCX-derived pages are guard-safe (no `.pdf`/`.docx` substring) and drop the `.md.md` double suffix; source-page / stub-concept-claim / index writers anchored to `PROJECT_ROOT` (matches `ingest_image_stub`, makes them CWD-independent + unit-testable). **(Fix 2 — `chunk.py`)** heading-only splitting left the heading-less PDF prose as one 191k-char chunk — unsearchable (the sentence-transformer embeds only its first ~256 tokens; BM25 dilutes to noise). Added size-bounded sub-splitting: any heading-section over `MAX_CHUNK_CHARS` (1000, sized to the embedder window) is packed on line boundaries into ≤1000-char chunks, single over-long lines hard-wrapped. Small sections keep the **legacy chunk_id** (no `::idx::` segment), so ~94% of the corpus is byte-identical and reuses stored embeddings. Ingested source/concept/claim pages are gitignored by design ("private — generated from other projects"); they live in the working tree + local indices, so only the reusable code + tests + this log are committed.
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/ingest.py`, `scripts/chunk.py`, `tests/test_chunk.py` (+3), `tests/test_ingest.py` (+2). Local (gitignored) state: `raw/papers/claudes-constitution_webPDF_26-02.02a.pdf`, `wiki/sources/claudes-constitution_webPDF_26-02_02a.md` (full text, 194 KB), `wiki/concepts/AutoConcept-…`, `wiki/claims/Claim-…`, rebuilt `data/search.sqlite` (203 constitution chunks, corpus max chunk 191,874→1000 chars) + `data/graph.sqlite`.
- **Verification:** `pytest` → **262 passed, 0 failed** (+5). `eval_search --hybrid --min-top3 0.6` before/after the chunker change: **identical** (top-1 67%, top-3 100%, MRR 0.778) — the ~6% re-split touched no gold answer. Frozen provenance golds unchanged (2C 86% recall, 100% completeness/hash, 0% raw leak, 100% graph). Live search: constitution is the top hit for "Claude's character and values", "how should Claude handle harmful requests", "broad safety and honesty" with `hybrid_score` 0.94 (kw 1.0 / sem 0.86) and relevant snippets. `public_repo_guard.py` + `git diff --check` clean.
- **Follow-ups:** Claims/concepts/summaries for the constitution are stub-only until a real provider (`ZURVAN_LLM_PROVIDER=anthropic`/`ollama`) runs `extract.py` — do not treat the stub concept/claim as evidence. Pytest still writes real entries to `wiki/log.md` (test-isolation leak; reverted before commit) — worth fixing by monkeypatching `LOG_FILE`. `MAX_CHUNK_CHARS` (1000) can be retuned if a future eval regression appears.

### 2026-07-04 (Australia/Sydney) — Agent memory in practice (E1–E5)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Phase 20 — make Zurvan a working memory for LLM agents: real embeddings, write-back habit, real-project federation, global CLI, staleness detection
- **Summary:** **(E1)** Semantic search is now real: `embed.py` caches SentenceTransformer models and accepts explicit provider/model; `search_hybrid` embeds queries with the provider/model **stored in the index** (mixing providers gave meaningless cosine scores); `db_path` params throughout; `test_hybrid_search` uses a temp index so pytest can no longer silently downgrade the real index to mock; `.claude/settings.json` sets `sentence_transformers`/`all-MiniLM-L6-v2`; live index rebuilt (3409 embeddings, 65s); eval gate passes (top-1 67%, top-3 100%, MRR 0.778). **(E2)** Write-back habit: `agent prime` closes with the decision/claim/question/postedit nudge; CLAUDE.md documents what to record. **(E3)** Federation refactored to run Zurvan's own retriever/graph in-process against each registered project's root (`_search_internal root=`, `expand_graph_context db_path=`) — previously it spawned `python -c "from scripts...."` inside the target repo, requiring every project to embed the whole engine; knowledge-only projects (wiki/+docs/) are now first-class, keyword federation needs no index, federated paths are project-relative. Registered real projects `[private-project]` ([private-project]) and `[private-project]` ([private-project]) with minimal wiki scaffolds; verified 3-project federated search, no warnings. **(E4)** `scripts/zurvan` self-locating wrapper symlinked to `/opt/homebrew/bin/zurvan` — callable from any project. **(E5)** `agent prime` now reports index freshness (newest file mtime vs newest `indexed_at`): fresh / STALE+command / missing.
- **Files Changed:** `scripts/embed.py`, `scripts/hybrid_search.py`, `scripts/rebuild_search_index.py`, `scripts/context_export.py`, `scripts/cross_project_search.py`, `scripts/cross_project_context.py`, `scripts/evidence_collect.py`, `scripts/agent_workflow.py`, `scripts/zurvan` (new), `.claude/settings.json`, `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/ENVIRONMENT.md`, `docs/API.md`, tests: `test_hybrid_search.py` (rewritten, temp-index), `test_cross_project_search.py` (rewritten, knowledge-only e2e), `test_cross_project_context.py`, `test_agent_workflow.py` (+3), `test_cli.py` (+1). Machine state: `~/.zurvan/projects.json` (+[private-project], +[private-project]), `/opt/homebrew/bin/zurvan` symlink, minimal `wiki/` scaffolds in [private-project] and [private-project] (+`AGENTS.md` stub in [private-project]).
- **Verification:** `pytest` → 257 passed, 0 failed (+6 over Phase 19). Index confirmed `sentence_transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`; semantic scores meaningful on conceptual queries; `eval_search --hybrid --min-top3 0.6` passes. Frozen provenance golds untouched (score committed traces). Federated `search-all` returned results from all 3 valid projects, 0 warnings. `zurvan version/prime/search --json` verified from `/private/tmp` via the PATH wrapper (anaconda `python3` carries `sentence_transformers`+`mcp`). `public_repo_guard.py` + `git diff --check` clean per commit.
- **Follow-ups:** [private-project]/[private-project] wikis are scaffolds — content accrues via the write-back habit; build their search indexes (`zurvan --project <name> index search`) once they have content to make hybrid federation live. If PATH ever resolves a `python3` without `sentence_transformers`, queries fall back to mock **with a printed warning** (degraded, visible). `raoof128` registry entry is stale/invalid — deregister or repair someday.

### 2026-07-04 (Australia/Sydney) — Agent UX + R4 retrieval quality
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Phase 19 (agent ergonomics) + R4 (retrieval quality) — 7 features landed as separate commits
- **Summary:** Made Zurvan cheaper and more effective for LLM agents. **(19a)** `CLAUDE.md` agent quickstart (commands, hard rules, change protocol). **(19b)** Restructured `AGENTS.md`: rules + standing invariants first; all but the two most recent entries archived verbatim in `docs/agents-history.md` (44/44 headings verified preserved). **(19c)** `--json` output for `search`, `context --format json`, `eval search`, `eval provenance` — compact machine-parseable payloads (repo-relative paths, single-line snippets, `trace_path`); output format only, scoring asserted unchanged in tests. **(19d)** New MCP tool `zurvan_read_page` wrapping the existing `resource_file` safety gate (12 tools total) so agents keep search compact and open only needed pages. **(19e)** Incremental index rebuild: embeddings reused for unchanged `chunk_id`s (content-hashed), guarded by a provider/model probe — re-index of an unchanged corpus computes 1 embedding instead of ~3.4k. **(19f)** `zurvan agent prime`: topic-free ~300-token orientation card, wired as a Claude Code SessionStart hook in `.claude/settings.json`. **(R4a)** FTS5 `tokenize='porter unicode61'` — documented ranking change: hybrid eval top-1 33%→67%, top-3 100%→100% (0.6 gate passes), MRR 0.556→0.778; closes the R1B lexical miss ("citations" vs "Citation": kw 0.000→0.862 live). **(R4b)** `context --max-per-source` (default 2, 0 disables) caps a dominating source's chunks before budgeting, recovering the R1B cutoff near-misses; excess recorded in traces as `dropped` with reason `source_dedupe`.
- **Files Changed:** `CLAUDE.md` (new), `AGENTS.md`, `docs/agents-history.md` (new), `scripts/context_export.py`, `scripts/eval_search.py`, `scripts/eval_provenance.py`, `scripts/cli.py`, `scripts/mcp_tools.py`, `scripts/mcp_server.py`, `scripts/rebuild_search_index.py`, `scripts/agent_workflow.py`, `.claude/settings.json` (new), `docs/API.md`, tests: `test_context_export.py` (+5), `test_eval_search.py` (+1), `test_eval_provenance.py` (+1), `test_mcp_tools.py` (+3), `test_hybrid_search.py` (+2), `test_agent_workflow.py` (+1), `test_cli.py`
- **Verification:** `pytest` → 251 passed, 0 failed (was 238; +13). Frozen provenance golds re-scored unchanged after both R4 changes: 2C 86% recall, 1B 79%, 0% raw leak / 100% hash / 100% completeness / 100% graph. `eval_search --hybrid --min-top3 0.6` passes (top-3 100%). Embedding reuse measured live: 3400 reused / 1 computed on an unchanged corpus. `agent prime` output < 4k chars (asserted). `public_repo_guard.py` passed per commit; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Follow-ups:** R4a/R4b improve the *live* retriever; the frozen 1B gold's 79% is a historical artifact of its committed traces and intentionally not re-run against the new index. A future R4 benchmark should re-trace the 12 frozen queries as a new artifact (2C/1B style) to quantify the recall gain. R3 remains unbuilt.

### 2026-07-04 (Australia/Sydney)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Full project audit — file-by-file bug hunt + fixes (no R3, no ranking/schema/eval-scoring changes)
- **Summary:** Audited every script in `scripts/`. Fixed 9 confirmed defects: (1) env: `starlette` 1.3.1 (pulled in by `mcp`) removed the `on_startup` kwarg that `fastapi` 0.111 still passes — review workbench + 8 tests were broken; upgraded fastapi to 0.139.0 and raised the `requirements.txt` floor to `>=0.133.0` (first release without a starlette upper bound). (2) `hybrid_search`: unquoted FTS5 terms crashed on bareword keywords (`AND`/`OR`/`NOT`/`NEAR`) — terms now quoted; identical match set/ranking for plain tokens. (3) `chunk.py`: CWD-relative scan meant `zurvan index search` from any foreign CWD (e.g. MCP) silently wiped and rebuilt an EMPTY index — now anchored to `PROJECT_ROOT` with repo-relative identity; chunk IDs verified byte-identical from repo root. (4) `extract.py`: claim tags were joined with a literal `\n` producing malformed YAML (confirmed in `wiki/claims/claim-dummy-001.md`, also repaired); missing `makedirs` for `data/extractions/` + `wiki/summaries/` crashed on fresh trees. (5) `memory.add_claim`: CWD-relative source check broke `zurvan_claim_add` via MCP — resolves against `PROJECT_ROOT`. (6) `cross_project_search`: query was f-string-interpolated into generated Python (quote-in-query breakage / code injection) and spawned bare `python`; query now travels via argv, `sys.executable` everywhere (also `cross_project_context`, `evidence_collect`); `snippet` field was always `None`, now real text. (7) `cli.py`: subprocess commands (`audit`, `index`, `eval search/validate-gold`, `graph rebuild/export`) used bare `python`, CWD-relative paths, and swallowed child exit codes — `zurvan eval search --min-top3` could never fail; new `_run_script()` anchors to `PROJECT_ROOT` and propagates return codes. (8) `context_export._save_synthesis`: keyword-mode `--save` wrote absolute machine paths into tracked wiki frontmatter — now repo-relative. (9) `review_audit`: corrupt-report early return omitted `stats`, crashing the dashboard index rebuild with KeyError.
- **Files Changed:** `requirements.txt`, `scripts/hybrid_search.py`, `scripts/chunk.py`, `scripts/extract.py`, `scripts/memory.py`, `scripts/cross_project_search.py`, `scripts/cross_project_context.py`, `scripts/evidence_collect.py`, `scripts/cli.py`, `scripts/context_export.py`, `scripts/review_audit.py`, `wiki/claims/claim-dummy-001.md`, `tests/test_chunk.py` (+2), `tests/test_hybrid_search.py` (+2), `tests/test_cross_project_search.py` (+1), `tests/test_memory.py` (+1), `tests/test_extract.py` (new, 2), `tests/test_cli.py` (+2)
- **Verification:** `pytest` → 238 passed, 0 failed (baseline was 220 passed / 1 failed / 7 errors; +10 new regression tests). Frozen provenance golds re-scored unchanged: 2C 86% recall, 1B 79%, both 0% raw leak / 100% hash / 100% completeness / 100% graph. `eval_search --hybrid --min-top3 0.6` → top-3 100%. Rebuilt search index chunk-ID set verified identical to pre-fix (only diff = test-injected dummy row + the repaired claim file's own root chunk). Functional: `search_hybrid("search AND rescue")` no longer raises; `scan_markdown_files()` finds 847 files from `/private/tmp` (was 0). `public_repo_guard.py` passed; `git diff --check` clean; `compileall` OK.
- **Follow-ups:** `ingest.py`/`extract.py`/`audit_wiki.py` still write via CWD-relative `wiki/` when run directly (safe via `zurvan` CLI now that subprocesses anchor `cwd=PROJECT_ROOT`) — migrate incrementally. `report_export`'s extra redaction pass mangles 64-char content hashes into `[REDACTED_TOKEN]` (safety-over-fidelity; review someday). Legacy `query.py`/`rebuild_index.py` remain CWD-relative Phase-1 tools.
