### 2026-06-14 (Australia/Sydney)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Phase R1B follow-ups — recall miss-analysis (#2) + scorer negative-branch tests (#1)
- **Summary:** Two post-PR analysis tasks on `phase-r1b-provenance-events`; no R3, and no change to retrieval ranking/scoring/indexing/graph/schema, nor to the frozen 2C pilot or 1B real-run traces. **(#2)** Categorised every missed expected source behind the 79% recall: 6 missed links / 23 across 4 of 12 queries. Read-only finding — **none** is a missing, unindexed, or sub-threshold source; all 6 rank below the query's `limit`. Breakdown: 2 cutoff near-misses (recoverable, one compounded by single-source chunk domination taking 3/5 slots), 2 annotation/query-design mismatches (retriever arguably correct → 79% understates quality), 1 over-broad gold on an ambiguous query, 1 genuine lexical gap (no FTS stemming: query "citations" ≠ heading "Citation"). **(#1)** Added two scorer negative-branch tests proving the conditional discriminates — `retrieval.fusion` is not demanded for a keyword-only gold, and graph is not scored for a non-graph gold, while the same fusion-/graph-less trace fails when those events *are* expected. Verified by mutation (made the scorer unconditional → both tests red → reverted).
- **Files Changed:** `docs/evaluation/provenance-real-run-1b-miss-analysis-2026-06-14.md` + `.json` (new, analysis only), `tests/test_eval_provenance.py` (+2 negative-branch tests). No production code changed.
- **Verification:** `pytest` → 228 passed (+2). New tests proven to bite via scorer mutation (red), then green after `git checkout`. Frozen 1B gold re-scores 79%/100%/100% unchanged. `public_repo_guard.py` passed; `git diff --check` clean; `compileall` OK.
- **Follow-ups:** Stemming/Porter tokenizer (or plural query expansion) would close the one genuine lexical miss; source-dedupe before budgeting would recover the cutoff near-misses — both out of scope here (no ranking/indexing change). Re-annotate `real-evidence-report-02` (gold expects review docs; query points at reports/evidence). R3 still frozen.

### 2026-06-14 (Australia/Sydney)
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Phase R1B — provenance event enrichment (`phase-r1b-provenance-events`)
- **Summary:** Raised the RQ1 provenance ceiling from a 3-event subset toward audit completeness. Added one genuinely-new event type `retrieval.fusion` (records existing hybrid weights `{fts:0.6, embedding:0.4}` + per-chunk ranks; observe-only, hybrid only) and populated the existing `context.assembled.dropped` with real `budget` drops + explicit `dropped_reason: no_dropped_context` for the empty case. ### 2026-09-28h (Australia/Sydney) — session record: two pushes close Z0; the write-back itself was bypassing the barrier
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** `scripts/agent_workflow.py` (`agent_postedit` appends through `append_file_safely`), `tests/test_agent_workflow.py` (+1 barrier test; the old unit test moved into a store and an operation, assertions unchanged); records.
- **Pushes, each at Raouf's explicit request with `ZURVAN_ALLOW_PUSH=1` for that push only:** `c72479c..fbf7139` (11 commits: the session record, the Z0 step-6 plan and its gauntlet, Z0 step 6) and `fbf7139..9bd4637` (6 commits: Z0 step 7, closure). Both fast-forwards of the exact tree that passed the full suite; `public_repo_guard` passed before each. A third, 2026-09-29 at Raouf's request: `9bd4637..5983d5f` (the postedit fix and this record), after a re-run of the full suite on that tip (1345 passed / 0 failed).
- **Found while writing back: `zurvan agent postedit` bypassed the mutation barrier.** It appended to `wiki/log.md` with a raw `open(..., "a")`, so the write-back CLAUDE.md prescribes at the end of every session left `knowledge status` reporting unrecorded drift (revision 250), which the next managed write mints as EXTERNAL_OBSERVED — a Zurvan writer recorded as if an editor had touched the wiki. Now one MANAGED revision with no drift, by a test through the real CLI (red before the fix). The drift already on the live store is recorded honestly by the next write as EXTERNAL_OBSERVED; it is not rewritten.
- **Same bypass, filed not fixed:** `zurvan session start|close` (`scripts/session.py` writes a session page and `wiki/log.md` raw) and `scripts/rebuild_index.py` (`wiki/index.md`) — neither activates eagerly the way ingest and extract do (28c).
- **Boundary is not configured on this machine.** `~/.zurvan/boundary` is absent, so `check.sh` gate 0 and the pre-push scan both report NOT CONFIGURED; the two pushes were checked by `public_repo_guard` only. Earlier sessions ran it (27/27 red-team). Filed.
- **A process lesson, saved to memory:** a scripted anchor edit duplicated ~590 lines of `cli.py` while every test stayed green; only `git diff --stat` caught it. Compare the diff size with the plan before committing any scripted edit.
- **Verification.** pytest **1345 passed / 4 skipped / 0 failed**; `public_repo_guard` passed; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Next.** Z1 (retire the burned retrieval instrument; content-addressed edge ids); route `session` and `rebuild_index` through the barrier; re-configure Boundary; the deferred minors in 28f and 28g; the public mirror (repository name still to be chosen).

### 2026-09-29 (Australia/Sydney) — overnight: another session's decision was mis-attributed, then hand-fixed outside the barrier
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** records only, no code.
- **What the ledger shows.** At 07:54:03 another session's `decision add` committed revision 259 with `store_project=zurvan`; the page (a client-project decision) was stamped with the active workspace because the command ran without `--project <client-slug>`. At 07:54:07 the page's `project:` field was corrected to the client's slug by a direct edit, outside the mutation barrier, so `knowledge status` reported unrecorded drift.
- **How it is recorded.** The next managed write mints that edit as its own EXTERNAL_OBSERVED revision (§5.2) — the honest label for a change made outside the barrier. The page now names the client; the ledger row for revision 259 still says `zurvan`. History is not rewritten; this entry is the reconciliation of the two.
- **The rule that would have avoided both:** `zurvan --project <slug> decision add …` or `decision add --project <slug>` (CLAUDE.md). Filed as a question: whether `decision add` should refuse, or warn, when run for a subject whose slug differs from the active workspace.
- **Also:** the third 2026-09-28 session push (`9bd4637..5983d5f`) is recorded in 28h.

### 2026-10-01 (Australia/Sydney) — Z1a built: retrieval observability without leaks; ranking unchanged vs BASE
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** branch `z1a-observability` (from `a79b26b`): `scripts/trace_schema.py`, `trace_validate.py`, `trace_writer.py`, `context_export.py`, `hybrid_search.py`, `eval_search.py`, `cli.py`; new `scripts/eval_datasets.py`, `scripts/eval_runs.py`, `eval/datasets.json`; 10 test files (8 new); spec §3.2 and status; CLAUDE.md bullet. Plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-29-z1a-observability.md`, executed task by task with an implementer and a reviewer per task (subagents on Sonnet; Task 9 inline).
- **Summary:**
  - Traces are schema v2 with `trace_mode`. `--trace` defaults to `summary`: no raw query (sha256 and length only), no snippets, no headings or node titles, no Markdown twin. `--trace-mode full` is explicit, and `protected_eval` is forced inside a protected evaluation and is never a CLI choice. v1 traces still read and validate unchanged.
  - Trace files are 0600 in 0700 dirs. Every write prunes untracked traces (30 days, newest 500), never a git-tracked one, and a prune failure never fails the write.
  - Stage timings (`fts`, `embed_query`, `vector_scan`, `hydrate`, `fuse`, `total`) and `search --explain` are observation only.
  - `evaluate()` returns per-item rows by index and a timing-free `result_digest`. `failing_queries` became `failing_items`, and the gold loader's malformed-line warning no longer echoes the line.
  - Dataset registry: the burned 50-item instrument is `RETIRED` by whole-file digest. `eval search --save-run` and `eval movers` refuse HOLDOUT/RESERVE: before writing, and by re-resolving the current registry state, so a dataset sealed after a run was saved stays sealed. Movers also refuse runs with different item sets. `eval reproduce` compares digests on one coherent snapshot.
- **Review fixes beyond the plan text:** a prune race could fail a landed write (Task 2). Movers trusted a stale stored state and compared truncated runs (Task 7). The closure matrix overclaimed: it now maps every gate to the tests that demonstrate it, and `LIVE_PROOF` names gates 1, 4, 7 and 8 as needing the live run.
- **Live store reconciled first (2026-10-01):** rev 301 had UNRECORDED drift, because 77 `wiki/decisions` pages were rewritten in one batch at 07:34 outside the barrier. The graph was 6 revisions stale. Two dead rebuild snapshots were reclaimed by `knowledge gc`. The search rebuild's capture minted the drift as rev 302 EXTERNAL_OBSERVED, and search (297,992 chunks, 21 embeddings recomputed) and graph (11,484 nodes) were rebuilt to 302 CLEAN, with no drift.

  - `trace prune --dry-run`: 0 removed, 33 tracked kept.

  - Worktree suite: 15 failed / 1401 passed. All 15 are environmental, identical before and after every task, and caused by the worktree lacking the untracked `.private-projects`, wiki pages and graph DB. Gate 8 closes only on a 0-failed suite on merged `main`.

### 2026-10-01 (Australia/Sydney) — Z1a hardening: the deferred review minors
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** `scripts/trace_writer.py`, `trace_validate.py`, `cli.py`, `eval_datasets.py`, `eval_runs.py`, `eval_search.py`; new `tests/test_z1a_hardening_{traces,eval}.py`; `tests/test_trace_modes.py`, `tests/test_instruments.py`. Done inline, test first (red, then green).

  - **Traces:**
    - Rewriting a trace id in summary or protected mode removes its earlier full Markdown twin.
    - An existing trace file is made 0600 (`fchmod`) before new bytes land.
    - Negative `--days`/`--keep` are refused.
    - The validator now refuses a v1 trace that claims a mode, as the loader already did.
    - The frozen-trace test requires every committed trace to be v1 with zero issues.
    - New end-to-end tests check default summary traces for `search_memory`, `export_context` and `context --trace-mode`. Further tests cover a `git ls-files` timeout inside a repo and the prune CLI.
  - **Eval:**
    - Item rows are an allow-list (DEV and RETIRED only).
    - `DatasetInfo` validates its state and digest when it is built, and a digest registered twice is refused.
    - Rows are returned beside the metrics, never inside them.
    - A sealed dataset reports failure counts but no `failing_items`.
    - `--save-run` refuses a sealed set before evaluating, with exit 2.
    - The runs dir is created 0700.
    - `eval movers` exits 2 on an invalid registry state.
    - `reproduce` refuses fewer than 2 runs or an empty gold set. It reports `items` and `snapshot_id` instead of a path that has already been released.
    - The instrument-tracking test is pinned to the repo, so it no longer passes vacuously from another cwd.
- **Not fixed, with reasons:**
  - **#8:** `git ls-files` runs on every write; about 7 ms, measured.
  - **#10 and #15:** cosmetic.
  - **#13:** the spec allows result ids in summary.
  - **#14:** negligible, and the plan mandated it.
  - **#19:** keyword-mode absolute paths. Normalising them would change keyword-mode rankings, which needs its own before/after eval.
  - **#23a:** a run that fails `--min-top3` exits before it is saved. Changing that changes gate exit behaviour.
- **Verification:**
  - No ranking or indexing change: metric computation is untouched, and the RETIRED live instrument still lists its failing items.
  - The affected suites in the worktree: 188 passed. The 4 failures are environmental, because the worktree lacks the untracked wiki pages and graph DB.
  - On merged `main` (`1cb538f`): full suite **1445 passed, 4 skipped, 0 failed** (8m55s).

### 2026-10-01 (Australia/Sydney) — waiting items: the 07:34 drift explained, barrier bypasses closed, gauntlet isolated, Boundary rebuilt
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** `scripts/memory.py`, `cli.py`, `session.py`, `rebuild_index.py`, `run_reliability_gauntlet.py`, `eval_search.py`. New tests: `test_decision_set_project.py`, `test_session_index_barrier.py`, `test_gauntlet_isolation.py`. Adapted: `test_session.py`, `test_check_gate_index_rebuild.py`. Docs: the mirror plan (name) and the Z1a execution record (#19). Done inline, test first.
- **The 07:34 drift (rev 301/302), root cause found.** Acme session `ed112e1a` met `project: "unknown"` on decisions it had just added. It had run `decision add` from `Job_Projects/Acme`, which isn't registered, so resolution failed closed, as designed. It then fixed the pages with `sed -i` in `wiki/decisions`, outside the ledger. In the same run it changed every `"acme"` to `"Acme"`, which isn't a valid slug. `sed -i` rewrote all 77 `acme-*` pages, 52 of them byte-identical, so they all share one mtime.
  - The gap was that Zurvan had no managed way to correct a subject.
  - **Now:** `zurvan decision set-project PAGE... --project <slug>` validates the slug the same way `decision add` does. It rewrites only the frontmatter line, checks every page before writing any, and declares the subject to the ledger: one revision, MANAGED.
  - `decision add` now prints a warning naming that command whenever it stamps `unknown`.
  - The 77 pages were relabelled to `acme` (Raouf's choice) through the new command.
- **Barrier bypasses (filed 2026-09-28), closed:**
  - `session start|close` and `index rebuild` now write through `safe_write`. `index rebuild` runs in-process inside the CLI operation and targets the store, not the cwd.
  - The reliability gauntlet (check.sh gate 3) copies its `raw/` sources into a throwaway store and runs ingest, extract and audit there. It used to ingest 5 sources into the live corpus on every gate.
- **Z1a #19:** keyword-mode eval rows are store-relative, so the result digest is the same from any checkout. Before/after on the live wiki: top-10 lists for all 50 items byte-identical (`ee674618aef5`), metrics identical (top-1 0.0, top-3 0.0, MRR 0.0022, 50 failures), so ranking is unchanged.
- **Boundary configured (Raouf's choice of protected set, revised once):**
  - **First build (wrong), now removed:** every gitignored `wiki/sources` page plus `sources/` (10,585 files, 225 MB; 12,676,118 fingerprints; red-team 27/27). The batch audit blocked 67 of 453 tracked files, including LICENSE and README. `wiki/sources` holds ingested public papers and Zurvan's own docs, so their text matched the repo: the same ingestion round-trip AGENTS.md records for the invalid 11/200 measurement. My protected-set estimate (11 MB) came from the spec's 2026-09-15 sample and was wrong.
  - **Current store:** INSTRUMENT is the 50-item gold set. PRIVATE is the repo's `sources/`. That gives 2 origins, 782 fingerprints, 100 literals, health `healthy`. Red-team 27/27. `boundary_guard` (check.sh gate 0) is clean.
  - **Audit: 22 of 453 tracked files blocked, all D2 INSTRUMENT literals.**
    - Gold item 0's query, in 5 files: the frozen eval and trace artifacts the mirror plan already allows for.
    - The `notes` annotation field of items 7, 20, 29, 37, 39 and 46, in 17 files. These notes are 10-30 characters of generic phrasing, so registering them as literals is too broad (`_instrument_literals` registers every string field of at least 8 characters).
    - Filed, not changed: limiting the registration to `query` changes what Boundary protects and needs a Boundary re-measurement. Until then, a manual `boundary scan-diff` that stages one of those files will block.
  - **`boundary audit` is unusably slow at scale:** it calls `scan_blob` per file, and each call re-reads the protected state. It ran more than 1h30 against the large store before I stopped it. `batch_scan` (one frozen view) did the same 453 files in 2 minutes.
- **Public mirror:** named `Raoof128/zurvan`. Not built or published. Plan Tasks 1-3 remain, and Task 4 needs an explicit yes.
- **MCP:** `zurvan_decision_add` gained `project=`. Its result names the stamped project and, for `unknown`, the managed fix. The new tool `zurvan_decision_set_project` does that fix (the e2e smoke now requires it). The same Acme session sed-edited a decision again at 14:14 (rev 317) because the MCP tool could not name a subject. An MCP server started earlier must be restarted to get the parameter.
- **Suite regressions found and fixed:**
  - `test_shelled_commands_stay_silent_on_success` mocked `subprocess` and ran `index rebuild`, which is now in-process. It regenerated the LIVE `wiki/index.md` once (rev 312, managed, no drift, identical to `zurvan index rebuild`). It now uses `audit`.

- **Acme pages:** the 77 `acme-*` decisions were relabelled `"Acme"` -> `"acme"` with `decision set-project` (rev 311, MANAGED, no drift).

  - An earlier run (1456 passed, 1 error) tripped the live-index guard. Acme session `2d85a7de` ran `zurvan index search && graph rebuild` mid-suite. That was a concurrent writer, not a test, and that run is not counted.

    - A predeclaration cannot name its own commit.
    - Authoring writes plaintext items to a path that Time Machine backs up.
  - 23 orchestrator rulings so far.
- **Files:** `docs/superpowers/progress/2026-10-02-z1b-plan-gauntlet.md` (progress record: method, task states, discrepancies D1-D16, added refusal codes, rulings R1-R23, remaining steps), `AGENTS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/agents-history.md`.

**Raouf:**

Raouf asked what the session-start warnings `Deny rule Write(//…) never matches · use Edit(//…)` were, then to fix them. No engine, ranking, indexing, eval or frozen-artifact change; no Z1b gate status changes.
- **Summary:** Claude Code checks every file write against `Edit(...)` rules; a `Write(...)` deny rule is never consulted. Each of the four sealed roots (`/Users/zurvaneval`, `/Library/ZurvanEval/corpus`, `/Library/ZurvanEval/stage`, `/Users/zurvancandidate`) already carried an `Edit(//<root>/**)` rule beside the dead `Write` one, so coverage is unchanged: `Read`, `Edit`, `Read(<root>)` and `Bash(*<root>*)` remain for all four. `scripts/z1b_client.py` `_DENY_TOOLS` is now `("Read", "Edit")`, so `client_deny_rules` stops emitting the dead rule and a regenerated settings file cannot bring the warning back. The seal-check (`_client_deny_rules_present`) already required only Read and Edit; it is untouched. The OS permission (Z1b gate 1) remains the wall.
- **Files:** `.claude/settings.json`, `scripts/z1b_client.py`, `tests/test_z1b_repo_guard.py`, `tests/test_z1b_client.py`, AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG.md, `docs/agents-history.md` (rotation). ### 2026-11-07 (Australia/Sydney) — Package A whole-branch review: claim/summary → source graph edge restored; order-dependent snapshot test isolated
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** the whole-branch review of `package-a` (`main...package-a`) that Package A's follow-ups name, plus that entry's "fix the pre-existing `eval/datasets.json` fixture failure". No ranking, indexing, embedding or frozen-artifact change; nothing merged, nothing pushed.
- **Review:** one independent read-only reviewer (Sonnet) over the code diff. Nothing blocking outright; F5's "ranking unchanged" claim held on reading (`FusionPolicy.score` = `kw*k + (1-kw)*s`, `kw=0.2`, same as the Rust scorer; `MIN_HYBRID_SCORE` 0.2 with strict `>`). One real regression, verified here before fixing: F1 made `source_id` content-identified (`<stem>-<hash12>`, plan line 154, intended), but `graph_build.py` linked claims through `wiki/sources/{source_id}.md` while ingest still names the source page `wiki/sources/<stem>.md`, so `add_edge` silently dropped every new claim's and summary's `derived_from` edge.
- **Fix:** `graph_build.extract_edges` links through `source_file` (the ingested basename, sanitised the way `ingest.source_page_stem` does) and still through `source_id` for pages written before F1, de-duplicated; `extract.summary_page()` writes the summary page and now records `source_file`. Live impact: none — 0 pages in `wiki/claims`/`wiki/summaries` carry `source_file`/`source_hash`, i.e. no extraction ran on this checkout since F1.
- **Snapshot test:** `test_measurement_snapshot.py::test_eval_search_scores_against_the_index_it_is_given` now pins `eval_datasets.REGISTRY` to the tracked `eval/datasets.json` (the convention `test_z1b_promotion.py` already uses). It failed alone before (`FileNotFoundError` for `<tmp>/eval/datasets.json`: `REGISTRY` is bound at import from a then-patched `PROJECT_ROOT`) and passes alone after; its file 23 passed. The import-time binding in `scripts/eval_datasets.py` is unchanged.
- **Files:** `scripts/graph_build.py`, `scripts/extract.py`, `tests/test_graph_build.py`, `tests/test_measurement_snapshot.py`, AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG.md, `docs/agents-history.md` (rotation).
- **Verification:** test-first — `test_an_extracted_claim_derives_from_its_ingested_source_page` failed on the missing edge (`assert [] == ['derived_from']`) and `test_an_extraction_summary_derives_from_its_ingested_source_page` on the missing writer; after the fix graph/extract tests 29 passed. Full suite: 3382 passed / 1 failed / 8 skipped in 37:30 (3 more passed than on 2026-11-05: the new graph tests). The 1 failure is reported as failed: `test_z1b_seal_live.py::test_t17_live_gate2_author_identity_cannot_read_the_keys_or_sealed_sets`, `sudo -n` exits 1 with empty output (no cached operator sudo), the same pre-existing failure as 2026-10-06, 2026-10-20e and 2026-11-04b. `public_repo_guard.py` and `git diff --check` before commit.
- **Follow-ups (review findings not fixed, none blocking):** (a) F4 identity stamp is the probe's, while vector reuse checks only provider+model (`rebuild_search_index.py:348` vs `:266`); (b) typed embedding errors unhandled in `agent preflight` (`agent_workflow.py:48`), `zurvan_context` errors where search/orient degrade, sqlite connection left open on embed failure (`hybrid_search.py:126-133`); (c) F1 gaps vs the roadmap gate: same-hash re-extraction rewrites claim text without comparing it, open questions re-appended on every run, `ExtractionConflict` returns normally, heading uses the sanitised id where the plan said raw; (d) Rust: `compute_graph` opens by path unpinned (tiny window), `GENERATION_CHANGED` wording on graph failure, graph tools carry no generation, orient header not renamed; (e) Rust fusion constants not tested against `fusion_policy.py`; (f) `status_index.py --check` not wired into `check.sh` though the plan said it would be; stale `_stamp_index` docstring; unused `embed.identity_for`; corrupt `embedding_identity` JSON raises uncaught. Then Raouf decides the merge, rebuild `~/.zurvan/bin/zurvan-mcp` (installed binary is 20 Oct; the 4 Nov target build predates the F6 commit).

### 2026-11-07d (Australia/Sydney) — Package B (Z1b closure) attempted: gate 4 FAILED on a sweep false positive, fixed prospectively; gate 9 criterion met with a blocking root finding; Z1b NOT closed
**Raouf:**
- **Scope:** Package B, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-02-z1b-sealed-eval.md` Task 19 Steps 4–7, authorised by Raouf ("do all one by one until this phase of z1b is completely done"; the charged gate 4 + gate 5 runs confirmed explicitly). Nothing pushed.
- **Step 4 (operator, Raouf at a terminal):** `sudo scripts/eval_seal/install.sh a6b1c92…` → broker current (`stale: false`, installed tree `8ac1ca7a…`); `tests/test_z1b_seal_live.py` and `tamper_drill.sh` run by Raouf ("all done"; their output was not shown to this session, so their pass is Raouf's report, not verified here). `seal-check`: all 23 checks true.
- **Gate 8: PASS.** `eval/z1b/manifest.json` (suite `bedc01c1dce53b46`, seq 3): HOLDOUT 160 (16 strata × 10), RESERVE 80 (16 × 5), `short: []`, `burn_ledger: []`.
- **Gate 4: FAILED, reported as failed.** Four attempts, three refused before any charge (budget stayed 8): (1) `CANDIDATE_NOT_FROZEN` — `git status --porcelain` counts untracked files (another session's spec draft, `.claude/worktrees/`); the client was then run from a clean detached worktree `.worktrees/z1b-gate4` at `a6b1c92` (`REPO` is the running code's checkout). (2) `SCAN_INCOMPLETE` — the eval identity could not read `data/traces` (0700, its setup ACL absent; when it was lost is unknown) and three October `.worktrees` transcript dirs (`mcp-hybrid`, `mcp-perf`, `rust-mcp`); granted the setup ACL (`user:zurvaneval allow read,list,search,readattr,file_inherit,directory_inherit`, as owner, no sudo) on those and `wiki/traces`; free `broker scan` then clean. (3) `DECLARATION_REQUIRED` — holdout needs `--declare eval/z1b/declarations/closure-baseline.json`. (4) **Charged run** (23:26–23:28): `SANDBOX_VIOLATION`, budget 8 → **7**, **1 open incident**; holdout/reserve still 160/80 (consistent with few or no items sent). The cached second run and gate 5 were NOT run: an open incident refuses every run, and its id is visible to the operator only.
- **Root cause (deterministic false positive, reproduced):** `sweep.sweep_roots` added the candidate's macOS per-user folder (`/private/var/folders/6f/hdz9…`, created 2026-10-05 by the first host-side `probe`) as a strict root, and macOS keeps `0/ C/ T/ X/` there owned by the candidate, so `find_candidate_leftovers` was non-empty on every run regardless of the candidate. Same functions on the dev uid: 64,417 "leftovers". **Second blocker found:** `/Library/ZurvanEval/broker/images/guest.qcow2` does not exist — tonight's install passed no `--guest-image`, and no golden `.qcow2` was found on the dev side — so a real run cannot boot its VM; the false positive masked that failure.
- **Fix (prospective; the failed run stays failed):** `5f437d6`, `36a2887`, `8f5036f` (Sonnet subagent, reviewed here, plus two findings from the automated security review). Strict roots (`/tmp`, `/private/tmp`, `/private/var/tmp`, `/Users/Shared`, candidate home) unchanged. The darwin per-user roots are baseline-diffed: snapshot before `launch_candidate`, violation on any new or changed candidate-owned path; signature `(ino, mtime_ns, size, mode, ctime_ns, opaque)` — listable dirs ino+mode only (a deleted child is not a violation), a dir the eval identity cannot list (the candidate's 0700 `C/`, `T/`) keeps its full times so a hidden child still shows; ctime catches a same-size rewrite with mtime restored. Candidate processes are checked before AND after the filesystem sweep. A failed baseline or any sweep error is a violation (fail closed). 37 new tests in `tests/test_z1b_sweep_baseline.py`, failing before / passing after.
- **Gate 9: criterion met; blocking finding.** Fresh Sonnet red-team with the plan's brief verbatim: reported FAILURE (no holdout/reserve query, target or canary obtained; `BAD_ARGS` on crafted args, env not preserved through sudo, closed-schema output). Transcript `~/.zurvan/reports/z1b-redteam-2026-11-07.jsonl` (0600). `broker scan` afterwards found no hit and no unreadable target (no new incident; it returns `INTEGRITY_INCIDENT` only because the gate-4 incident is still open). **But** the red-team found, and this session verified, passwordless root for the dev account: `sudo -n -l` lists `(ALL) NOPASSWD: /opt/homebrew/bin/brew, /usr/local/bin/brew, /usr/sbin/softwareupdate, /bin/launchctl`, and `/opt/homebrew/bin/brew` is owned by `raoof.r12` (dir `drwxrwxr-x raoof.r12:admin`). Root reads the age key and sealed sets, so the seal's premise (dev identity has no root) does not hold. Not exploited.
- **Verdict (Step 7): Z1b is NOT closed.** Gate 4 failed (and must be re-run on a broker with the fix), gate 5 not run, gate 9's root finding must be removed. Budget 7 of 8.
- **Files:** `scripts/eval_seal/{broker,sweep}.py`, `tests/test_z1b_broker.py`, `tests/test_z1b_sweep_baseline.py`, AGENTS.md, CHANGELOG.md, `docs/agents-history.md` (rotation). Outside the repo: ACLs on `data/traces`, `wiki/traces` and three `~/.claude/projects/*Zurvan--worktrees-*` dirs; `~/.zurvan/reports/z1b-redteam-2026-11-07.jsonl`; worktree `.worktrees/z1b-gate4`.
- **Verification:** full suite on main at `8f5036f`: 3455 passed / 1 failed / 8 skipped in 36:17 (37 more passed than 2026-11-07c: the new sweep tests). The 1 failure is reported as failed: `test_z1b_seal_live.py::test_t17_live_gate2_author_identity_cannot_read_the_keys_or_sealed_sets` (no cached operator sudo), the same pre-existing failure. `public_repo_guard.py` and `git diff --check` before commit.
- **Follow-ups (operator, in order):** (1) remove the `brew`/`launchctl`/`softwareupdate` NOPASSWD lines from sudoers (`sudo visudo -f` the file that holds them); (2) locate or re-bake the golden image (design §7m) and reinstall the broker at the new HEAD with `--guest-image <path>` (the fix also makes the broker STALE); (3) `sudo -u zurvaneval /Library/ZurvanEval/broker/bin/zurvan-broker ack-incident <id>` at a terminal (id in the broker's private incidents dir); (4) re-run gate 4 (`--declare eval/z1b/declarations/closure-baseline.json`, twice, from a clean checkout) and gate 5 (`--commit c753dbf…`, run from a checkout whose HEAD matches the installed broker). If the macOS VM host processes legitimately touch the candidate's `T/`/`C/`, the new check fails closed and needs a design decision. Consider a seal-check item that refuses when the dev identity holds any NOPASSWD root grant.
